# Browse (Plan 3c) Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Players search Modrinth from the desktop app and install what they find.
- **Modpacks:** become a new instance through the existing 3b `pack_add` path.
- **Mods, resource packs and shaders:** go into a compatible instance, or a new one, together with their required dependencies (and Iris or Oculus for shaders).
- **Where:** a global Browse on the rail, a scoped Browse from an instance's Mods screen, and a project page with gallery and a sanitized description.

**Architecture:**
- **Core:** a new `launcher-core::browse` module holds everything: a small Modrinth API client (`api`), compatibility rules (`compat`), search and project fetch (`search`, `project`), installed-content identification by sha1 (`identify`), the dependency planner (`plan`, written against a `Catalog` trait so it is unit-testable), the create-new content writer (`install`) and the image cache (`images`). A `Browse` service ties them together.
- **App:** `launcher-app` reaches the core through a new `Browser` trait that `Runner` hands out (`CoreBrowser` real, `FakeBrowser` in tests). `AppCore` keeps install plans server-side by id, enforces the busy rules, and turns images into data URLs. Content installs are not runs: they have their own guard, and Play refuses while one is in flight.
- **Wiring and UI:** `src-tauri` forwards the commands and opens links with the opener plugin's Rust API. The React UI adds a `browse` route, the Browse screen, the project page, the install flow and success notices, styled through `/impeccable`.

**Tech Stack:**
- Rust stable (edition 2021): tokio, serde, reqwest; no new core dependencies (`sha2`, `serde_json`, `percent-encoding` are already there).
- UI: React 19, TanStack Query (`useInfiniteQuery`), zod 4, i18next, Vitest + Testing Library.
- New UI dependencies: `react-markdown` `^10.1.0` and `remark-gfm` `^4.0.1`.

**Spec:**
- `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-11-browse-design.md` (owner-approved 2026-10-11). Every task argues from it; section numbers below (§3.4 etc.) point into it.
- Parent specs: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-09-launcher-core-design.md`, `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-10-launcher-desktop-ui-design.md`, `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-10-pack-sync-design.md` (§4 holds the 3b decisions this builds on).
- Design system: `DESIGN.md` and the contract `.impeccable/surfaces/ui-src-app-tsx.md`.
- The 3b plan (`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-11-pack-sync-desktop.md`) shows the app and UI code this plan builds on.

## Global Constraints

- **License:** GPL-3.0-only. No Modrinth or Mojang branding or assets: show only the projects' own data (title, summary, icon, gallery). Nothing is ported, so `NOTICE` doesn't change.
- **Crate boundaries:**
  - `launcher-core` and `launcher-app` never depend on Tauri or a UI crate.
  - `src-tauri` is wiring only (`test = false`). Every behavior and its test lives in `launcher-app` or `launcher-core`.
- **Contract:**
  - `ui/src/ipc/contract/*.json` is generated: `UPDATE_CONTRACT=1 cargo test -p launcher-app --test contract`. Never hand-edit it.
  - The zod schemas in `ui/src/ipc/schema.ts` are strict objects and must match the Rust serde shapes exactly.
- **Strings:**
  - Every UI string goes in both `ui/src/i18n/en.json` and `ui/src/i18n/es.json`. Spanish is neutral Latin American with informal "tú". Copy is short and casual.
  - Every new `ErrorCode` gets `errors.<code>.message` in both files (`i18n.test.ts` enforces it).
- **Modrinth:**
  - API base `https://api.modrinth.com/v2`; images only from `https://cdn.modrinth.com`. Every request carries the launcher's User-Agent (`launcher_core::http::client`).
  - Stay far below about 300 requests a minute: the UI debounces typing by 300 ms and keeps queries for 5 minutes.
  - Ids and slugs pass `sources::valid_modrinth_id` before they go into a URL.
- **Security:**
  - The webview never sends a download URL or hash: installs go by `planId` only.
  - Images reach the webview only as `data:` URLs in `<img>`, so an SVG can't run script. The CSP is unchanged.
  - Markdown renders without raw HTML; links open only when they are `https://` (checked in `launcher-app`, opened from Rust).
  - Content files are created, never overwritten; names are one safe path component (`fsutil::safe_component`) with the kind's extension.
  - Messages name hosts, never full URLs.
- **Design system:** one lit button per view (on the project page: **Install**; in a dialog: its primary). Lime lamp for "installed"/selection, blue for progress. Pixel-stepped corners, hard-offset shadows, rows not rounded cards. New surfaces are styled through `/impeccable` (Task 22).
- **Coordination:** the join-by-link launcher plan may land first. It edits the `perland://` handler, run phases, the New-instance Pack link tab, pack art and the banner: don't redesign those. Rebase onto `main` before the PR and **regenerate** the contract instead of hand-merging it.
- **Stable APIs:** changes are additive, except two deliberate breaks:
  - `CoreRunner::new` takes a `Browse` as its fourth argument. Its only caller is `src-tauri`.
  - `Runner` gains `fn browser(&self) -> Arc<dyn Browser>`. Its implementors are `CoreRunner` and `FakeRunner`.
- **Heavy tests:** fuzzing, stress and load tests run only in CI, **never on this PC**. Local test inputs stay tiny (a handful of files, bodies of a few bytes; caps are tested with `*_limited` variants and small limits).
- **Commits:** every commit message ends with exactly the trailer `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>` (not Sonnet). Subagent prompts must repeat this.
- **Before each commit:**
  - `cargo fmt --all`
  - `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings`
  - `cargo test --workspace`
  - When `ui/` changed, also: `pnpm --filter ui build`, `pnpm -r lint`, `pnpm -r typecheck`, `pnpm -r test`.
- **Machine notes:**
  - No Python on this PC; use Node 24 for scripts.
  - Windows Smart App Control sometimes blocks a fresh unsigned test exe (os error 4551). That's environmental; rerun.
  - The owner's terminal is PowerShell, so give them PowerShell commands.

## Review Focus

1. **A version whose files don't suit the launcher.** The primary file is a `-sources.jar` next to the real jar, a resource pack's primary is a `.jar`, the URL is plain `http://`, or there's no sha1.
   - **Expected:** the right file is chosen when one exists; otherwise that version doesn't "fit" and the next one is picked, never a broken download.
   - **Pinned by:** `usable_file_prefers_the_right_extension_and_refuses_unsafe_files` (Task 2).
2. **A diamond of dependencies** (A needs B and C; B and C both need D), and a cycle (B needs A).
   - **Expected:** D is planned once, the cycle ends, and the plan lists A, B, C, D in resolve order.
   - **Pinned by:** `a_diamond_and_a_cycle_plan_each_project_once` (Task 6).
3. **A double click on Install** (two `browse_install` calls with one `planId`).
   - **Expected:** the first installs; the second gets `content_plan_expired`; files are written once.
   - **Pinned by:** `a_plan_installs_once` (Task 14).
4. **Odd search input:** quotes, `"]`, unicode, 500 characters, a category like `x"],["project_type:mod`.
   - **Expected:** the query is trimmed to 100 characters and sent as a query parameter; bad categories are dropped; facets stay valid JSON with exactly the intended groups.
   - **Pinned by:** `facets_ignore_hostile_categories_and_trim_the_query` (Task 3).
5. **A hostile file name from Modrinth:** `../mods/x.jar`, `CON.jar`, `a/b.jar`, `x.jar.` or a name without the kind's extension.
   - **Expected:** refused with `mod_name_invalid` before anything is downloaded or written; nothing appears outside the kind's folder.
   - **Pinned by:** `hostile_file_names_are_refused_before_downloading` (Task 7).

### Task 1: Error codes and the Modrinth API client

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/launcher-core/src/error.rs` (the `error_codes!` list and a test)
- Modify: `crates/launcher-core/src/paths.rs` (`images()`)
- Modify: `crates/launcher-core/src/lib.rs` (`pub mod browse;`)
- Create: `crates/launcher-core/src/browse/mod.rs`, `crates/launcher-core/src/browse/api.rs`
- Create: `crates/launcher-core/tests/browse.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces `ErrorCode::{BrowseRateLimited, ContentNotFound, ContentIncompatible, ContentDependencyMissing, ContentConflict, ContentNeedsLoader, ContentAlreadyInstalled, ContentBusy, ContentInstalling, ContentPlanExpired, ImageRefused, LinkRefused}` (snake_case on the wire: `browse_rate_limited`, …, `link_refused`).
- Produces `LauncherDirs::images(&self) -> PathBuf` (`<root>/cache/images`).
- Produces in `launcher_core::browse::api`:
  - `pub const MAX_JSON_BYTES: usize`
  - `pub struct Api` with `new(http: reqwest::Client, base: impl Into<String>) -> Api`, `with_limit(self, usize) -> Api`, `url(&self, path: &str, query: &[(&str, String)]) -> Result<reqwest::Url>`, `get<T>(&self, Url) -> Result<T>`, `post<T, B>(&self, Url, &B) -> Result<T>`, and the endpoints `project(&self, id) -> Result<ApiProject>`, `members(&self, id) -> Result<Vec<ApiMember>>`, `versions(&self, id, loaders: &[&str], game_version: Option<&str>) -> Result<Vec<ApiVersion>>`, `version(&self, id) -> Result<ApiVersion>`, `version_files(&self, sha1s: &[String]) -> Result<HashMap<String, ApiVersion>>`, `search(&self, query: &[(&str, String)]) -> Result<ApiSearch>`, `categories(&self) -> Result<Vec<ApiCategory>>`.
  - `pub fn not_found() -> LauncherError` (`content_not_found`).
  - Raw types (all `pub` fields): `ApiProject`, `ApiGallery`, `ApiLicense`, `ApiMember`, `ApiUser`, `ApiVersion`, `ApiFile`, `ApiHashes`, `ApiDependency`, `DependencyType`, `ApiSearch`, `ApiHit`, `ApiCategory`.

Create `crates/launcher-core/tests/browse.rs`. Later tasks append to it; the helpers at the top are shared.

```rust
//! Browse (Plan 3c) against a wiremock Modrinth: the API client, search, projects, identify,
//! the content writer, images and the `Browse` service.
use launcher_core::{
    browse::api::{Api, DependencyType},
    download::hash::{sha1_hex, sha512_hex},
    ErrorCode,
};
use serde_json::{json, Value};
use wiremock::{
    matchers::{body_json, method, path, query_param},
    Mock, MockServer, ResponseTemplate,
};

fn api(server: &MockServer) -> Api {
    Api::new(
        launcher_core::http::client("test").unwrap(),
        format!("{}/v2", server.uri()),
    )
}

async fn serve_json(server: &MockServer, at: &str, body: Value) {
    Mock::given(method("GET"))
        .and(path(at))
        .respond_with(ResponseTemplate::new(200).set_body_json(body))
        .mount(server)
        .await;
}

/// How many requests the server saw for `at`.
async fn hits(server: &MockServer, at: &str) -> usize {
    server
        .received_requests()
        .await
        .unwrap_or_default()
        .iter()
        .filter(|r| r.url.path() == at)
        .count()
}

/// A Modrinth version whose one file is `body`, served at `<server>/files/<file_name>`.
fn version_json(
    server: &MockServer,
    id: &str,
    project: &str,
    game_versions: &[&str],
    loaders: &[&str],
    file_name: &str,
    body: &[u8],
) -> Value {
    json!({
        "id": id,
        "project_id": project,
        "name": format!("{project} {id}"),
        "version_number": id,
        "version_type": "release",
        "game_versions": game_versions,
        "loaders": loaders,
        "date_published": "2026-01-01T00:00:00Z",
        "files": [{
            "url": format!("{}/files/{file_name}", server.uri()),
            "filename": file_name,
            "primary": true,
            "size": body.len(),
            "hashes": { "sha1": sha1_hex(body), "sha512": sha512_hex(body) }
        }],
        "dependencies": []
    })
}

fn project_json(id: &str, slug: &str, title: &str, project_type: &str) -> Value {
    json!({
        "id": id,
        "slug": slug,
        "title": title,
        "description": format!("{title} summary"),
        "body": format!("# {title}\n\nLong text."),
        "project_type": project_type,
        "categories": ["optimization", "fabric"],
        "client_side": "required",
        "server_side": "optional",
        "downloads": 1234,
        "followers": 56,
        "icon_url": "https://cdn.modrinth.com/data/x/icon.png",
        "license": { "id": "LGPL-3.0-only", "name": "GNU LGPL 3.0" },
        "source_url": "https://github.com/example/x",
        "issues_url": null,
        "wiki_url": null,
        "gallery": []
    })
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn rate_limits_say_how_long_to_wait() {
    let server = MockServer::start().await;
    for (id, header, expected) in [("aaa", Some("12"), "12"), ("bbb", Some("999"), "60"), ("ccc", None, "10")] {
        let mut response = ResponseTemplate::new(429);
        if let Some(h) = header {
            response = response.insert_header("x-ratelimit-reset", h);
        }
        Mock::given(method("GET"))
            .and(path(format!("/v2/project/{id}")))
            .respond_with(response)
            .mount(&server)
            .await;
        let error = api(&server).project(id).await.unwrap_err();
        assert_eq!(error.code, ErrorCode::BrowseRateLimited, "{id}");
        assert_eq!(error.params["seconds"], expected, "{id}");
    }
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn missing_projects_and_server_trouble_have_their_own_codes() {
    let server = MockServer::start().await;
    Mock::given(method("GET"))
        .and(path("/v2/project/gone"))
        .respond_with(ResponseTemplate::new(404))
        .mount(&server)
        .await;
    Mock::given(method("GET"))
        .and(path("/v2/project/broken"))
        .respond_with(ResponseTemplate::new(503))
        .mount(&server)
        .await;
    assert_eq!(
        api(&server).project("gone").await.unwrap_err().code,
        ErrorCode::ContentNotFound
    );
    let trouble = api(&server).project("broken").await.unwrap_err();
    assert_eq!(trouble.code, ErrorCode::HostTrouble);
    assert_eq!(trouble.params["status"], "503");
    assert!(!trouble.message.contains("/v2/"), "messages name hosts only");
    assert_eq!(
        api(&server).project("../x").await.unwrap_err().code,
        ErrorCode::ContentNotFound
    );
    assert_eq!(hits(&server, "/v2/project/../x").await, 0);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn an_unreachable_host_is_host_unreachable() {
    let closed = Api::new(launcher_core::http::client("test").unwrap(), "http://127.0.0.1:9/v2");
    let error = closed.project("abc").await.unwrap_err();
    assert_eq!(error.code, ErrorCode::HostUnreachable);
    assert_eq!(error.params["host"], "127.0.0.1");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn junk_or_oversized_answers_are_unexpected_data() {
    let server = MockServer::start().await;
    Mock::given(method("GET"))
        .and(path("/v2/project/junk"))
        .respond_with(ResponseTemplate::new(200).set_body_string("<html>not json</html>"))
        .mount(&server)
        .await;
    serve_json(&server, "/v2/project/big", project_json("big", "big", "Big", "mod")).await;
    assert_eq!(
        api(&server).project("junk").await.unwrap_err().code,
        ErrorCode::MetaUnexpected
    );
    assert_eq!(
        api(&server).with_limit(16).project("big").await.unwrap_err().code,
        ErrorCode::MetaUnexpected
    );
    assert_eq!(api(&server).project("big").await.unwrap().title, "Big");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn versions_send_loader_and_game_filters_as_json_arrays() {
    let server = MockServer::start().await;
    Mock::given(method("GET"))
        .and(path("/v2/project/sodium/version"))
        .and(query_param("loaders", r#"["quilt","fabric"]"#))
        .and(query_param("game_versions", r#"["1.20.1"]"#))
        .respond_with(ResponseTemplate::new(200).set_body_json(json!([version_json(
            &server, "v1", "sodium", &["1.20.1"], &["fabric"], "sodium.jar", b"jar"
        )])))
        .mount(&server)
        .await;
    let list = api(&server)
        .versions("sodium", &["quilt", "fabric"], Some("1.20.1"))
        .await
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(list.len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(list[0].files[0].filename, "sodium.jar");
    assert!(list[0].dependencies.is_empty());
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn version_files_posts_sha1_hashes() {
    let server = MockServer::start().await;
    let hash = sha1_hex(b"jar");
    let mut version = version_json(&server, "v1", "sodium", &["1.20.1"], &["fabric"], "sodium.jar", b"jar");
    version["dependencies"] = json!([{ "project_id": "other", "version_id": null, "dependency_type": "incompatible" }]);
    Mock::given(method("POST"))
        .and(path("/v2/version_files"))
        .and(body_json(json!({ "hashes": [hash.clone()], "algorithm": "sha1" })))
        .respond_with(ResponseTemplate::new(200).set_body_json(json!({ hash.clone(): version })))
        .mount(&server)
        .await;
    let found = api(&server).version_files(&[hash.clone()]).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(found[&hash].project_id, "sodium");
    assert_eq!(
        found[&hash].dependencies[0].dependency_type,
        DependencyType::Incompatible
    );
}
```

Create `crates/launcher-core/src/browse/api.rs`:

```rust
//! Modrinth's public API v2 for Browse. Plain requests with the launcher's User-Agent (the
//! client comes from `http::client`), so a rate limit or a missing project gets its own error
//! instead of the pack wording `sources::modrinth` uses.
use std::{collections::HashMap, fmt::Display};

use serde::{de::DeserializeOwned, Deserialize, Serialize};

use crate::{
    download::host_of,
    error::{ErrorCode, LauncherError, Result},
    sources::valid_modrinth_id,
};

/// No answer Browse asks for comes near this; a bigger one is refused rather than buffered.
pub const MAX_JSON_BYTES: usize = 8 << 20;
/// How long a rate-limited player waits when Modrinth doesn't say.
const DEFAULT_WAIT_SECS: u64 = 10;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize)]
pub struct ApiProject {
    pub id: String,
    pub slug: String,
    pub title: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub description: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub body: String,
    pub project_type: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub categories: Vec<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub client_side: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub server_side: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub downloads: u64,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub followers: u64,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub icon_url: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub license: Option<ApiLicense>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub source_url: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub issues_url: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub wiki_url: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub gallery: Vec<ApiGallery>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize)]
pub struct ApiLicense {
    pub id: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub name: Option<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize)]
pub struct ApiGallery {
    pub url: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub featured: bool,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub title: Option<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize)]
pub struct ApiUser {
    pub username: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize)]
pub struct ApiMember {
    #[serde(default)]
    pub role: String,
    pub user: ApiUser,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub ordering: i64,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, Deserialize)]
pub struct ApiHashes {
    #[serde(default)]
    pub sha1: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub sha512: Option<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize)]
pub struct ApiFile {
    pub url: String,
    pub filename: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub primary: bool,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub size: u64,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub hashes: ApiHashes,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum DependencyType {
    Required,
    Optional,
    Incompatible,
    Embedded,
    #[serde(other)]
    Other,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize)]
pub struct ApiDependency {
    #[serde(default)]
    pub version_id: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub project_id: Option<String>,
    pub dependency_type: DependencyType,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize)]
pub struct ApiVersion {
    pub id: String,
    pub project_id: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub name: String,
    pub version_number: String,
    pub version_type: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub game_versions: Vec<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub loaders: Vec<String>,
    pub date_published: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub files: Vec<ApiFile>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub dependencies: Vec<ApiDependency>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize)]
pub struct ApiHit {
    pub project_id: String,
    pub project_type: String,
    pub slug: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub author: String,
    pub title: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub description: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub categories: Vec<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub display_categories: Vec<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub downloads: u64,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub follows: u64,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub icon_url: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub client_side: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize)]
pub struct ApiSearch {
    pub hits: Vec<ApiHit>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub offset: u32,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub total_hits: u32,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize)]
pub struct ApiCategory {
    pub name: String,
    pub project_type: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub header: String,
}

pub fn not_found() -> LauncherError {
    LauncherError::pack(ErrorCode::ContentNotFound, "That project wasn't found")
}

fn checked(id: &str) -> Result<&str> {
    if valid_modrinth_id(id) {
        Ok(id)
    } else {
        Err(not_found())
    }
}

fn unreachable(host: &str, e: reqwest::Error) -> LauncherError {
    LauncherError::network(ErrorCode::HostUnreachable, format!("Couldn't reach {host}"))
        .with_param("host", host)
        .with_fix("Check your internet connection")
        .with_detail(e.without_url())
}

fn dropped(host: &str, e: reqwest::Error) -> LauncherError {
    LauncherError::network(
        ErrorCode::ConnectionDropped,
        format!("Downloading from {host} was interrupted"),
    )
    .with_param("host", host)
    .with_detail(e.without_url())
}

fn unexpected(host: &str, detail: impl Display) -> LauncherError {
    LauncherError::loader(
        ErrorCode::MetaUnexpected,
        format!("Unexpected data from {host}"),
    )
    .with_param("host", host)
    .with_detail(detail)
}

/// A non-2xx answer: rate limits say how long to wait, 404/410 mean the project is gone, and
/// anything else is the host having trouble.
fn status_error(
    status: reqwest::StatusCode,
    headers: &reqwest::header::HeaderMap,
    host: &str,
) -> LauncherError {
    match status.as_u16() {
        429 => {
            let seconds = headers
                .get("x-ratelimit-reset")
                .and_then(|v| v.to_str().ok())
                .and_then(|v| v.trim().parse::<u64>().ok())
                .unwrap_or(DEFAULT_WAIT_SECS)
                .clamp(1, 60);
            LauncherError::network(
                ErrorCode::BrowseRateLimited,
                format!("Too many searches. Try again in {seconds} s"),
            )
            .with_param("seconds", seconds)
        }
        404 | 410 => not_found(),
        code => LauncherError::network(
            ErrorCode::HostTrouble,
            format!("{host} is having trouble ({status})"),
        )
        .with_param("host", host)
        .with_param("status", code)
        .with_fix("Try again in a few minutes"),
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Api {
    http: reqwest::Client,
    base: String,
    limit: usize,
}

impl Api {
    pub fn new(http: reqwest::Client, base: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self {
            http,
            base: base.into().trim_end_matches('/').to_string(),
            limit: MAX_JSON_BYTES,
        }
    }

    /// The same client with a smaller cap on answers (tests use a few bytes).
    pub fn with_limit(mut self, limit: usize) -> Self {
        self.limit = limit;
        self
    }

    pub fn url(&self, path: &str, query: &[(&str, String)]) -> Result<reqwest::Url> {
        let mut url = reqwest::Url::parse(&format!("{}/{path}", self.base))
            .map_err(|e| unexpected(&host_of(&self.base), e))?;
        if !query.is_empty() {
            let mut pairs = url.query_pairs_mut();
            for (key, value) in query {
                pairs.append_pair(key, value);
            }
        }
        Ok(url)
    }

    pub async fn get<T: DeserializeOwned>(&self, url: reqwest::Url) -> Result<T> {
        let host = host_of(url.as_str());
        let response = self
            .http
            .get(url)
            .send()
            .await
            .map_err(|e| unreachable(&host, e))?;
        self.read(response, &host).await
    }

    pub async fn post<T: DeserializeOwned, B: Serialize + ?Sized>(
        &self,
        url: reqwest::Url,
        body: &B,
    ) -> Result<T> {
        let host = host_of(url.as_str());
        let response = self
            .http
            .post(url)
            .json(body)
            .send()
            .await
            .map_err(|e| unreachable(&host, e))?;
        self.read(response, &host).await
    }

    async fn read<T: DeserializeOwned>(
        &self,
        mut response: reqwest::Response,
        host: &str,
    ) -> Result<T> {
        let status = response.status();
        if !status.is_success() {
            return Err(status_error(status, response.headers(), host));
        }
        if response
            .content_length()
            .is_some_and(|n| n > self.limit as u64)
        {
            return Err(unexpected(host, "the answer is too big"));
        }
        let mut body = Vec::new();
        while let Some(chunk) = response.chunk().await.map_err(|e| dropped(host, e))? {
            body.extend_from_slice(&chunk);
            if body.len() > self.limit {
                return Err(unexpected(host, "the answer is too big"));
            }
        }
        serde_json::from_slice(&body).map_err(|e| unexpected(host, e))
    }

    pub async fn project(&self, id: &str) -> Result<ApiProject> {
        self.get(self.url(&format!("project/{}", checked(id)?), &[])?)
            .await
    }

    pub async fn members(&self, id: &str) -> Result<Vec<ApiMember>> {
        self.get(self.url(&format!("project/{}/members", checked(id)?), &[])?)
            .await
    }

    /// A project's versions, filtered by Modrinth loader tags and one Minecraft version.
    pub async fn versions(
        &self,
        id: &str,
        loaders: &[&str],
        game_version: Option<&str>,
    ) -> Result<Vec<ApiVersion>> {
        let mut query = Vec::new();
        if !loaders.is_empty() {
            query.push(("loaders", serde_json::to_string(loaders).expect("strings")));
        }
        if let Some(mc) = game_version {
            query.push(("game_versions", serde_json::to_string(&[mc]).expect("strings")));
        }
        self.get(self.url(&format!("project/{}/version", checked(id)?), &query)?)
            .await
    }

    pub async fn version(&self, id: &str) -> Result<ApiVersion> {
        self.get(self.url(&format!("version/{}", checked(id)?), &[])?)
            .await
    }

    /// The versions files with these sha1s belong to, keyed by sha1. Unknown files are absent.
    pub async fn version_files(&self, sha1s: &[String]) -> Result<HashMap<String, ApiVersion>> {
        #[derive(Serialize)]
        struct Body<'a> {
            hashes: &'a [String],
            algorithm: &'static str,
        }
        self.post(
            self.url("version_files", &[])?,
            &Body {
                hashes: sha1s,
                algorithm: "sha1",
            },
        )
        .await
    }

    pub async fn search(&self, query: &[(&str, String)]) -> Result<ApiSearch> {
        self.get(self.url("search", query)?).await
    }

    pub async fn categories(&self) -> Result<Vec<ApiCategory>> {
        self.get(self.url("tag/category", &[])?).await
    }
}
```

            v("rel-old", "release", "2026-01-01T00:00:00Z", &["1.20.1"], &["fabric"], vec![file("a.jar", true)]),
            v("rel-mid", "release", "2026-02-01T00:00:00Z", &["1.20.1"], &["fabric"], vec![file("c.jar", true)]),
            v("alpha", "alpha", "2026-04-01T00:00:00Z", &["1.20.1"], &["fabric"], vec![file("d.jar", true)]),

        // A sources jar marked primary loses to the real jar.
        let sources_primary = v("x", "release", "d", &["1.20.1"], &["fabric"], vec![file("m-sources.jar", true), file("m.jar", false)]);
        assert_eq!(usable_file(&sources_primary, ContentKind::Mod).unwrap().filename, "m.jar");
        // A resource pack whose primary is a jar uses its zip.
        let rp = v("r", "release", "d", &["1.20.1"], &[], vec![file("r.jar", true), file("r.zip", false)]);
        assert_eq!(usable_file(&rp, ContentKind::ResourcePack).unwrap().filename, "r.zip");
        // Plain http, no sha1, unsafe names and the wrong extension are not usable.
        let mut http = file("h.jar", true);
        http.url = "http://example.com/h.jar".into();
        let mut no_sha1 = file("n.jar", true);
        no_sha1.hashes.sha1 = None;
        for bad in [http, no_sha1, file("CON.jar", true), file("x.jar.", true), file("a.txt", true)] {
            let name = bad.filename.clone();
            let version = v("b", "release", "d", &["1.20.1"], &["fabric"], vec![bad]);
            assert!(usable_file(&version, ContentKind::Mod).is_none(), "{name}");
            assert!(!fits(&version, ContentKind::Mod, &target("1.20.1", Some(LoaderKind::Fabric))), "{name}");
        }
        let mrpack = v("m", "release", "d", &["1.20.1"], &["fabric"], vec![file("Pack 1.0.mrpack", true)]);
        assert_eq!(usable_file(&mrpack, ContentKind::Modpack).unwrap().filename, "Pack 1.0.mrpack");
    }

    #[test]
    fn new_instances_take_the_newest_release_and_a_supported_loader() {
        let mc = ["1.20.1", "1.21.1", "24w14a", "1.21.2-pre1"];
        assert_eq!(newest_release(&mc.map(String::from)), Some("1.21.1"));
        assert!(is_release("1.20") && is_release("1.20.1") && !is_release("1.21-pre1") && !is_release("24w14a"));
        let forge_and_fabric = jar("a", &mc, &["forge", "fabric"]);
        assert_eq!(
            new_target(&forge_and_fabric, ContentKind::Mod),
            Some(target("1.21.1", Some(LoaderKind::Fabric)))
        );
        let neo = jar("n", &["1.21.1"], &["neoforge", "forge"]);
        assert_eq!(new_target(&neo, ContentKind::Mod).unwrap().loader, Some(LoaderKind::NeoForge));
        let datapack = jar("d", &["1.21.1"], &["datapack"]);
        assert_eq!(new_target(&datapack, ContentKind::Mod), None);
        let shader = v("s", "release", "d", &["1.21.1"], &["iris"], vec![file("s.zip", true)]);
        assert_eq!(new_target(&shader, ContentKind::Shader).unwrap().loader, Some(LoaderKind::Fabric));
        let rp = v("r", "release", "d", &["1.21.1"], &["minecraft"], vec![file("r.zip", true)]);
        assert_eq!(new_target(&rp, ContentKind::ResourcePack), Some(target("1.21.1", None)));
        let snapshots_only = jar("z", &["24w14a"], &["fabric"]);
        assert_eq!(new_target(&snapshots_only, ContentKind::Mod), None);
    }
}

```rust
//! Which Modrinth content fits which instance (spec §3.1): kinds, loader tags, picking a
//! version, choosing its file, and the instance a "New instance" install gets.
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

use super::api::{ApiFile, ApiVersion};
use crate::{
    download::Hashes,
    fsutil::safe_component,
    instances::GameSpec,
    meta::{catalog::compare_versions, loaders::LoaderKind},
    sources::check_pack_url,
};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum ContentKind {
    Modpack,
    Mod,
    ResourcePack,
    Shader,
}

impl ContentKind {
    pub const ALL: [ContentKind; 4] = [
        ContentKind::Modpack,
        ContentKind::Mod,
        ContentKind::ResourcePack,
        ContentKind::Shader,
    ];

    /// Modrinth's name for the kind (`project_type`).
    pub fn project_type(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            ContentKind::Modpack => "modpack",
            ContentKind::Mod => "mod",
            ContentKind::ResourcePack => "resourcepack",
            ContentKind::Shader => "shader",
        }
    }

    pub fn from_project_type(s: &str) -> Option<ContentKind> {
        Self::ALL.into_iter().find(|k| k.project_type() == s)
    }

    /// The game-folder subfolder its files go in; modpacks become instances instead.
    pub fn folder(self) -> Option<&'static str> {
        match self {
            ContentKind::Modpack => None,
            ContentKind::Mod => Some("mods"),
            ContentKind::ResourcePack => Some("resourcepacks"),
            ContentKind::Shader => Some("shaderpacks"),
        }
    }

    /// The file extension the launcher installs for the kind (lowercase, with the dot).
    pub fn extension(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            ContentKind::Modpack => ".mrpack",
            ContentKind::Mod => ".jar",
            ContentKind::ResourcePack | ContentKind::Shader => ".zip",
        }
    }
}

/// What content has to fit: an instance's Minecraft version and loader.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Target {
    pub minecraft: String,
    pub loader: Option<LoaderKind>,
}

impl Target {
    pub fn of(game: &GameSpec) -> Target {
        Target {
            minecraft: game.minecraft.clone(),
            loader: game.loader.as_ref().map(|l| l.kind),
        }
    }
}

/// The shader-pack tag every shader search and plan uses: Iris and Oculus both read these packs.
pub const SHADER_TAG: &str = "iris";

/// Tags Modrinth lists among a project's categories that are loaders, not categories.
pub const NON_CATEGORY_TAGS: [&str; 10] = [
    "fabric", "quilt", "forge", "neoforge", "iris", "optifine", "canvas", "vanilla", "minecraft",
    "datapack",
];

pub fn loader_tag(kind: LoaderKind) -> &'static str {
    match kind {
        LoaderKind::Fabric => "fabric",
        LoaderKind::Quilt => "quilt",
        LoaderKind::Forge => "forge",
        LoaderKind::NeoForge => "neoforge",
    }
}

/// The Modrinth loader tags an instance with `loader` can run: Quilt runs Fabric mods, and
/// NeoForge on 1.20.1 runs Forge mods.
pub fn loader_tags(loader: LoaderKind, minecraft: Option<&str>) -> Vec<&'static str> {
    match loader {
        LoaderKind::Quilt => vec!["quilt", "fabric"],
        LoaderKind::NeoForge if minecraft == Some("1.20.1") => vec!["neoforge", "forge"],
        other => vec![loader_tag(other)],
    }
}

/// The mod (by Modrinth slug) that runs shader packs on `loader`.
pub fn shader_mod(loader: Option<LoaderKind>) -> Option<&'static str> {
    match loader? {
        LoaderKind::Fabric | LoaderKind::Quilt | LoaderKind::NeoForge => Some("iris"),
        LoaderKind::Forge => Some("oculus"),
    }
}

/// 0 for releases, 1 for betas, 2 for anything else.
pub fn stability(version_type: &str) -> u8 {
    match version_type {
        "release" => 0,
        "beta" => 1,
        _ => 2,
    }
}

/// The file to install: the primary one when it has the kind's extension, else the first that
/// does (never a `-sources` jar). It must be https (loopback http in tests), carry a sha1 and
/// be one safe file name.
pub fn usable_file(v: &ApiVersion, kind: ContentKind) -> Option<&ApiFile> {
    let ext = kind.extension();
    let suits = |f: &ApiFile| {
        let lower = f.filename.to_ascii_lowercase();
        lower.ends_with(ext) && !lower.ends_with(&format!("-sources{ext}"))
    };
    let file = v
        .files
        .iter()
        .find(|f| f.primary && suits(f))
        .or_else(|| v.files.iter().find(|f| suits(f)))?;
    let sha1 = file
        .hashes
        .sha1
        .as_deref()
        .and_then(|s| Hashes::from_format("sha1", s));
    (safe_component(&file.filename) && check_pack_url(&file.url).is_ok() && sha1.is_some())
        .then_some(file)
}

/// The hashes the object cache verifies a file against: its sha1 and, when given, its sha512.
pub fn file_hashes(file: &ApiFile) -> Hashes {
    Hashes {
        sha1: file
            .hashes
            .sha1
            .as_deref()
            .and_then(|s| Hashes::from_format("sha1", s))
            .and_then(|h| h.sha1),
        sha512: file
            .hashes
            .sha512
            .as_deref()
            .and_then(|s| Hashes::from_format("sha512", s))
            .and_then(|h| h.sha512),
        ..Hashes::default()
    }
}

/// Does version `v` of a `kind` project run on `target` (spec §3.1)?
pub fn fits(v: &ApiVersion, kind: ContentKind, target: &Target) -> bool {
    if !v.game_versions.iter().any(|g| *g == target.minecraft) || usable_file(v, kind).is_none() {
        return false;
    }
    let has = |tag: &str| v.loaders.iter().any(|l| l == tag);
    match kind {
        ContentKind::Mod => target.loader.is_some_and(|loader| {
            loader_tags(loader, Some(&target.minecraft))
                .into_iter()
                .any(has)
        }),
        ContentKind::Shader => target.loader.is_some() && has(SHADER_TAG),
        ContentKind::ResourcePack => true,
        ContentKind::Modpack => false,
    }
}

/// Releases first, then betas, then alphas; the newest within each (spec §3.4).
pub fn pick(versions: &[ApiVersion], keep: impl Fn(&ApiVersion) -> bool) -> Option<&ApiVersion> {
    versions.iter().filter(|v| keep(v)).min_by(|a, b| {
        stability(&a.version_type)
            .cmp(&stability(&b.version_type))
            .then_with(|| b.date_published.cmp(&a.date_published))
    })
}

/// A release version number like `1.20` or `1.20.1` (no snapshots or pre-releases).
pub fn is_release(mc: &str) -> bool {
    let parts: Vec<&str> = mc.split('.').collect();
    (2..=3).contains(&parts.len())
        && parts
            .iter()
            .all(|p| !p.is_empty() && p.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_digit()))
}

pub fn newest_release(game_versions: &[String]) -> Option<&str> {
    game_versions
        .iter()
        .filter(|g| is_release(g))
        .max_by(|a, b| compare_versions(a, b))
        .map(String::as_str)
}

/// The instance a "New instance" install creates for `v`: its newest Minecraft release and the
/// first loader the launcher supports, in the order Fabric, Quilt, NeoForge, Forge. Shaders get
/// Fabric (for Iris), resource packs no loader.
pub fn new_target(v: &ApiVersion, kind: ContentKind) -> Option<Target> {
    usable_file(v, kind)?;
    let minecraft = newest_release(&v.game_versions)?.to_string();
    let has = |tag: &str| v.loaders.iter().any(|l| l == tag);
    let loader = match kind {
        ContentKind::Mod => Some(
            [
                LoaderKind::Fabric,
                LoaderKind::Quilt,
                LoaderKind::NeoForge,
                LoaderKind::Forge,
            ]
            .into_iter()
            .find(|k| has(loader_tag(*k)))?,
        ),
        ContentKind::Shader => {
            if !has(SHADER_TAG) {
                return None;
            }
            Some(LoaderKind::Fabric)
        }
        ContentKind::ResourcePack => None,
        ContentKind::Modpack => return None,
    };
    Some(Target { minecraft, loader })
}
```

```rust
//! Search and categories (spec §3.2).
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

use super::{
    api::{Api, ApiHit},
    compat::{loader_tags, ContentKind, NON_CATEGORY_TAGS, SHADER_TAG},
};
use crate::{
    error::{ErrorCode, LauncherError, Result},
    meta::loaders::LoaderKind,
};

pub const PAGE_SIZE: u32 = 20;
pub const MAX_QUERY_CHARS: usize = 100;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Default, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum SortIndex {
    #[default]
    Relevance,
    Downloads,
    Follows,
    Newest,
    Updated,
}

impl SortIndex {
    fn as_str(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            SortIndex::Relevance => "relevance",
            SortIndex::Downloads => "downloads",
            SortIndex::Follows => "follows",
            SortIndex::Newest => "newest",
            SortIndex::Updated => "updated",
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct SearchQuery {
    pub query: String,
    pub kind: ContentKind,
    pub minecraft: Option<String>,
    pub loader: Option<LoaderKind>,
    pub categories: Vec<String>,
    pub sort: SortIndex,
    pub offset: u32,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct SearchHit {
    pub project_id: String,
    pub slug: String,
    pub title: String,
    pub author: String,
    pub summary: String,
    pub icon_url: Option<String>,
    pub downloads: u64,
    pub follows: u64,
    pub categories: Vec<String>,
    pub kind: ContentKind,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct SearchPage {
    pub hits: Vec<SearchHit>,
    pub total: u32,
    pub offset: u32,
}

/// Category slugs as Modrinth writes them. Anything else is dropped before it reaches a facet.
pub fn valid_category(s: &str) -> bool {
    (1..=40).contains(&s.len())
        && s
            .bytes()
            .all(|b| b.is_ascii_lowercase() || b.is_ascii_digit() || b == b'-' || b == b'_')
}

fn valid_version(s: &str) -> bool {
    (1..=32).contains(&s.len())
        && s
            .bytes()
            .all(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || matches!(b, b'.' | b'-' | b'_' | b'+'))
}

/// Modrinth's facet filter: an AND of OR-groups, as JSON.
pub fn facets(q: &SearchQuery) -> Result<String> {
    let mut groups: Vec<Vec<String>> = vec![vec![format!("project_type:{}", q.kind.project_type())]];
    if let Some(mc) = &q.minecraft {
        if !valid_version(mc) {
            return Err(LauncherError::loader(
                ErrorCode::VersionInvalid,
                format!("\"{mc}\" isn't a valid version"),
            )
            .with_param("value", mc));
        }
        groups.push(vec![format!("versions:{mc}")]);
    }
    match (q.kind, q.loader) {
        (ContentKind::Shader, _) => groups.push(vec![format!("categories:{SHADER_TAG}")]),
        (ContentKind::Mod | ContentKind::Modpack, Some(loader)) => groups.push(
            loader_tags(loader, q.minecraft.as_deref())
                .into_iter()
                .map(|t| format!("categories:{t}"))
                .collect(),
        ),
        _ => {}
    }
    for category in q.categories.iter().filter(|c| valid_category(c)) {
        groups.push(vec![format!("categories:{category}")]);
    }
    groups.push(vec!["client_side!=unsupported".into()]);
    Ok(serde_json::to_string(&groups).expect("strings serialize"))
}

/// The query string for `GET /search`.
pub fn params(q: &SearchQuery) -> Result<Vec<(&'static str, String)>> {
    let query: String = q.query.trim().chars().take(MAX_QUERY_CHARS).collect();
    Ok(vec![
        ("query", query),
        ("facets", facets(q)?),
        ("index", q.sort.as_str().to_string()),
        ("offset", q.offset.to_string()),
        ("limit", PAGE_SIZE.to_string()),
    ])
}

/// The categories among Modrinth tags (loaders dropped). `project.rs` reuses it.
pub(crate) fn only_categories(tags: Vec<String>) -> Vec<String> {
    tags.into_iter()
        .filter(|c| !NON_CATEGORY_TAGS.contains(&c.as_str()))
        .collect()
}

fn hit(h: ApiHit) -> Option<SearchHit> {
    if h.client_side == "unsupported" {
        return None;
    }
    let kind = ContentKind::from_project_type(&h.project_type)?;
    let tags = if h.display_categories.is_empty() {
        h.categories
    } else {
        h.display_categories
    };
    Some(SearchHit {
        project_id: h.project_id,
        slug: h.slug,
        title: h.title,
        author: h.author,
        summary: h.description,
        icon_url: h.icon_url,
        downloads: h.downloads,
        follows: h.follows,
        categories: only_categories(tags),
        kind,
    })
}

pub async fn search(api: &Api, q: &SearchQuery) -> Result<SearchPage> {
    let page = api.search(&params(q)?).await?;
    Ok(SearchPage {
        hits: page.hits.into_iter().filter_map(hit).collect(),
        total: page.total_hits,
        offset: page.offset,
    })
}

/// The category slugs Modrinth offers for `kind`, sorted.
pub async fn categories(api: &Api, kind: ContentKind) -> Result<Vec<String>> {
    let mut names: Vec<String> = api
        .categories()
        .await?
        .into_iter()
        .filter(|c| c.project_type == kind.project_type() && valid_category(&c.name))
        .map(|c| c.name)
        .collect();
    names.sort();
    names.dedup();
    Ok(names)
}

```

        .and(query_param("loaders", r#"["fabric"]"#))
        .respond_with(ResponseTemplate::new(200).set_body_json(json!([old, forge_only, new])))
        .mount(&server)
        .await;
    let target = Target {
        minecraft: "1.20.1".into(),
        loader: Some(LoaderKind::Fabric),
    };
    let list = project::versions(&api(&server), "sodium", Some(&target)).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        list.iter().map(|v| v.id.as_str()).collect::<Vec<_>>(),
        vec!["new", "old"]
    );
    assert_eq!(list[0].size, Some(5));
    assert_eq!(serde_json::to_value(&list[0]).unwrap()["versionType"], "release");
}

```rust
//! A project's page and its versions (spec §3.3).
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

use super::{
    api::{not_found, Api, ApiVersion},
    compat::{fits, loader_tags, usable_file, ContentKind, Target, SHADER_TAG},
    search::only_categories,
};
use crate::error::Result;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum SideSupport {
    Required,
    Optional,
    Unsupported,
    #[serde(other)]
    Unknown,
}

fn side(s: &str) -> SideSupport {
    match s {
        "required" => SideSupport::Required,
        "optional" => SideSupport::Optional,
        "unsupported" => SideSupport::Unsupported,
        _ => SideSupport::Unknown,
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct GalleryImage {
    pub url: String,
    pub title: Option<String>,
    pub featured: bool,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct ProjectDetail {
    pub project_id: String,
    pub slug: String,
    pub title: String,
    pub author: Option<String>,
    pub summary: String,
    /// Markdown, as the author wrote it. The UI renders it without raw HTML.
    pub body: String,
    pub icon_url: Option<String>,
    pub downloads: u64,
    pub follows: u64,
    pub categories: Vec<String>,
    pub kind: ContentKind,
    pub client_side: SideSupport,
    pub server_side: SideSupport,
    pub license: Option<String>,
    pub source_url: Option<String>,
    pub issues_url: Option<String>,
    pub wiki_url: Option<String>,
    pub gallery: Vec<GalleryImage>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct VersionInfo {
    pub id: String,
    pub name: String,
    pub number: String,
    pub version_type: String,
    pub game_versions: Vec<String>,
    pub loaders: Vec<String>,
    pub date: String,
    /// The size of the file that would be installed.
    pub size: Option<u64>,
}

pub async fn project(api: &Api, id: &str) -> Result<ProjectDetail> {
    let p = api.project(id).await?;
    let kind = ContentKind::from_project_type(&p.project_type).ok_or_else(not_found)?;
    // The author is nice to have: a project owned by an organization may list nobody.
    let members = api.members(&p.id).await.unwrap_or_default();
    let author = members
        .iter()
        .find(|m| m.role.eq_ignore_ascii_case("owner"))
        .or_else(|| members.iter().min_by_key(|m| m.ordering))
        .map(|m| m.user.username.clone());
    let mut gallery: Vec<GalleryImage> = p
        .gallery
        .into_iter()
        .map(|g| GalleryImage {
            url: g.url,
            title: g.title,
            featured: g.featured,
        })
        .collect();
    gallery.sort_by_key(|g| !g.featured);
    Ok(ProjectDetail {
        project_id: p.id,
        slug: p.slug,
        title: p.title,
        author,
        summary: p.description,
        body: p.body,
        icon_url: p.icon_url,
        downloads: p.downloads,
        follows: p.followers,
        categories: only_categories(p.categories),
        kind,
        client_side: side(&p.client_side),
        server_side: side(&p.server_side),
        license: p.license.map(|l| l.name.unwrap_or(l.id)),
        source_url: p.source_url,
        issues_url: p.issues_url,
        wiki_url: p.wiki_url,
        gallery,
    })
}

/// Versions of `project_id`, asked for with the filters that fit `target`.
pub async fn fetch_versions(
    api: &Api,
    project_id: &str,
    kind: ContentKind,
    target: Option<&Target>,
) -> Result<Vec<ApiVersion>> {
    let minecraft = target.map(|t| t.minecraft.as_str());
    let tags: Vec<&str> = match (kind, target.and_then(|t| t.loader)) {
        (ContentKind::Mod, Some(loader)) => loader_tags(loader, minecraft),
        (ContentKind::Shader, _) => vec![SHADER_TAG],
        _ => vec![],
    };
    api.versions(project_id, &tags, minecraft).await
}

/// The versions the player can pick: those that fit `target` when there is one, else every
/// version with an installable file. Newest first.
pub async fn versions(api: &Api, id: &str, target: Option<&Target>) -> Result<Vec<VersionInfo>> {
    let p = api.project(id).await?;
    let kind = ContentKind::from_project_type(&p.project_type).ok_or_else(not_found)?;
    let list = fetch_versions(api, &p.id, kind, target).await?;
    let mut keep: Vec<&ApiVersion> = list
        .iter()
        .filter(|v| match target {
            Some(t) if kind != ContentKind::Modpack => fits(v, kind, t),
            _ => usable_file(v, kind).is_some(),
        })
        .collect();
    keep.sort_by(|a, b| b.date_published.cmp(&a.date_published));
    Ok(keep
        .into_iter()
        .map(|v| VersionInfo {
            id: v.id.clone(),
            name: v.name.clone(),
            number: v.version_number.clone(),
            version_type: v.version_type.clone(),
            game_versions: v.game_versions.clone(),
            loaders: v.loaders.clone(),
            date: v.date_published.clone(),
            size: usable_file(v, kind).map(|f| f.size),
        })
        .collect())
}
```

use std::{collections::BTreeMap, path::Path};

use launcher_core::{
    browse::identify::{identify, FileHashes},
    download::Hashes,
    sources::FileMode,
    sync::lockfile::{Managed, ManagedFile},
};

fn write(dir: &Path, rel: &str, body: &[u8]) {
    let path = dir.join(rel);
    std::fs::create_dir_all(path.parent().unwrap()).unwrap();
    std::fs::write(path, body).unwrap();
}

fn managed(paths: &[(&str, &[u8])]) -> Managed {
    Managed {
        version: Managed::VERSION,
        stamp: "s".into(),
        pack_version: "1".into(),
        choices: BTreeMap::new(),
        files: paths
            .iter()
            .map(|(p, body)| {

                    p.to_string(),
                    ManagedFile {
                        name: p.to_string(),
                        sha1: sha1_hex(body),
                        size: body.len() as u64,
                        mode: FileMode::Replace,
                        declared: Hashes::default(),
                    },

#[tokio::test]
async fn identify_names_projects_by_hash_and_remembers_hashes() {
    let server = MockServer::start().await;
    let game = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    write(game.path(), "mods/added.jar", b"added");
    write(game.path(), "mods/off.jar.disabled", b"off");
    write(game.path(), "mods/pack.jar", b"pack");
    write(game.path(), "mods/readme.txt", b"ignored");
    write(game.path(), "resourcepacks/faithful.zip", b"rp");

    let answer = json!({
        sha1_hex(b"added"): version_json(&server, "v1", "lithium", &["1.20.1"], &["fabric"], "added.jar", b"added"),
        sha1_hex(b"pack"): version_json(&server, "v2", "sodium", &["1.20.1"], &["fabric"], "pack.jar", b"pack"),
        sha1_hex(b"rp"): version_json(&server, "v3", "faithful", &["1.20.1"], &["minecraft"], "faithful.zip", b"rp")
    });
    Mock::given(method("POST"))
        .and(path("/v2/version_files"))
        .respond_with(ResponseTemplate::new(200).set_body_json(answer))
        .mount(&server)
        .await;
    let mut hashes = FileHashes::default();
    let installed = identify(&api(&server), game.path(), Some(&lock), &mut hashes)
        .await
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        installed.project_ids(),
        vec!["faithful".to_string(), "lithium".into(), "sodium".into()]
    );
    assert!(installed.projects["sodium"].managed);
    assert!(!installed.projects["lithium"].managed);
    assert_eq!(installed.projects["faithful"].path, "resourcepacks/faithful.zip");
    // The managed jar's sha1 came from the lockfile; the other three were hashed once.
    assert_eq!(hashes.hashed(), 3);
    identify(&api(&server), game.path(), Some(&lock), &mut hashes).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(hashes.hashed(), 3, "unchanged files aren't hashed again");
    let sent: Value = serde_json::from_slice(&server.received_requests().await.unwrap()[0].body).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(sent["hashes"].as_array().unwrap().len(), 4);

    let empty = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let before = hits(&server, "/v2/version_files").await;
    assert!(identify(&api(&server), empty.path(), None, &mut hashes).await.unwrap().projects.is_empty());
    assert_eq!(hits(&server, "/v2/version_files").await, before, "nothing to ask about");
}

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

Create `crates/launcher-core/src/browse/identify.rs`:

```rust
//! What content an instance already has, by Modrinth project (spec §3.5). Files are named by
//! their sha1 through `POST /version_files`, so nothing Browse installs needs a record.
use std::{
    collections::HashMap,
    path::{Path, PathBuf},
    time::SystemTime,
};

use super::api::{Api, DependencyType};
use crate::{
    download::hash::sha1_file,
    error::{LauncherError, Result},
    fsutil::path_key,
    sync::lockfile::Managed,
};

/// Content folders and the file endings Browse looks at in each.
const FOLDERS: [(&str, &[&str]); 3] = [
    ("mods", &[".jar", ".jar.disabled"]),
    ("resourcepacks", &[".zip"]),
    ("shaderpacks", &[".zip"]),
];

/// Remembers each file's sha1 by (path, size, modified time), so asking again only reads new
/// or changed files.
#[derive(Debug, Default)]
pub struct FileHashes {
    memo: HashMap<PathBuf, (u64, SystemTime, String)>,
    hashed: usize,
}

impl FileHashes {
    /// The file's sha1, or `None` when it is gone or isn't a file.
    pub async fn sha1(&mut self, path: &Path) -> Result<Option<String>> {
        let meta = match tokio::fs::metadata(path).await {
            Ok(meta) if meta.is_file() => meta,
            Ok(_) => return Ok(None),
            Err(e) if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => return Ok(None),
            Err(e) => return Err(e.into()),
        };
        let modified = meta.modified()?;
        if let Some((size, when, sha1)) = self.memo.get(path) {
            if *size == meta.len() && *when == modified {
                return Ok(Some(sha1.clone()));
            }
        }
        let owned = path.to_path_buf();
        let sha1 = tokio::task::spawn_blocking(move || sha1_file(&owned))
            .await
            .map_err(|e| LauncherError::from(std::io::Error::other(e)))??;
        self.hashed += 1;
        self.memo
            .insert(path.to_path_buf(), (meta.len(), modified, sha1.clone()));
        Ok(Some(sha1))
    }

    /// How many files were actually read (not answered from memory).
    pub fn hashed(&self) -> usize {
        self.hashed
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct InstalledProject {
    /// Where it is, relative to the game folder.
    pub path: String,
    /// Sync owns the file (a pack's mod).
    pub managed: bool,
    /// Projects the installed version says it can't run with.
    pub incompatible: Vec<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Installed {
    pub projects: HashMap<String, InstalledProject>,
}

impl Installed {
    pub fn project_ids(&self) -> Vec<String> {
        let mut ids: Vec<String> = self.projects.keys().cloned().collect();
        ids.sort();
        ids
    }
}

/// Every content file in the game folder, as canonical relative paths, sorted.
pub async fn content_files(game_dir: &Path) -> Result<Vec<String>> {
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    for (folder, endings) in FOLDERS {
        let mut entries = match tokio::fs::read_dir(game_dir.join(folder)).await {
            Ok(rd) => rd,
            Err(e) if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => continue,
            Err(e) => return Err(e.into()),
        };
        while let Some(entry) = entries.next_entry().await? {
            if !entry.file_type().await?.is_file() {
                continue;
            }
            let name = entry.file_name().to_string_lossy().into_owned();
            let lower = name.to_ascii_lowercase();
            if endings.iter().any(|e| lower.ends_with(e)) {
                out.push(format!("{folder}/{name}"));
            }
        }
    }
    out.sort();
    Ok(out)
}

pub async fn identify(
    api: &Api,
    game_dir: &Path,
    managed: Option<&Managed>,
    hashes: &mut FileHashes,
) -> Result<Installed> {
    let from_lockfile: HashMap<String, String> = managed
        .map(|m| {
            m.files
                .iter()
                .filter(|(_, f)| !f.sha1.is_empty())
                .map(|(p, f)| (path_key(p), f.sha1.to_ascii_lowercase()))
                .collect()
        })
        .unwrap_or_default();
    let mut by_hash: HashMap<String, (String, bool)> = HashMap::new();
    for rel in content_files(game_dir).await? {
        let (sha1, is_managed) = match from_lockfile.get(&path_key(&rel)) {
            Some(sha1) => (sha1.clone(), true),
            None => match hashes.sha1(&game_dir.join(&rel)).await? {
                Some(sha1) => (sha1, false),
                None => continue,
            },
        };
        by_hash.entry(sha1).or_insert((rel, is_managed));
    }
    if by_hash.is_empty() {
        return Ok(Installed::default());
    }
    let mut asked: Vec<String> = by_hash.keys().cloned().collect();
    asked.sort();
    let versions = api.version_files(&asked).await?;
    let mut installed = Installed::default();
    for (sha1, version) in versions {
        let Some((path, managed)) = by_hash.get(&sha1.to_ascii_lowercase()) else {
            continue;
        };
        let incompatible = version
            .dependencies
            .iter()
            .filter(|d| d.dependency_type == DependencyType::Incompatible)
            .filter_map(|d| d.project_id.clone())
            .collect();
        installed
            .projects
            .entry(version.project_id.clone())
            .or_insert(InstalledProject {
                path: path.clone(),
                managed: *managed,
                incompatible,
            });
    }
    Ok(installed)
}
```

                        id.to_string(),
                        InstalledProject {
                            path: format!("mods/{id}.jar"),
                            managed: *managed,
                            incompatible: incompatible.iter().map(|s| s.to_string()).collect(),
                        },
                    )
                })
                .collect(),
        }
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn a_mod_brings_its_required_dependencies_and_skips_what_is_installed() {
        let mut c = FakeCatalog::default();
        c.add("SOD", "Sodium", "mod", vec![fabric_mod("s1", "SOD", vec![
            dep("API", DependencyType::Required),
            dep("OPT", DependencyType::Optional),
            dep("EMB", DependencyType::Embedded),
            dep("PACKED", DependencyType::Required),
        ])]);
        c.add("API", "Fabric API", "mod", vec![fabric_mod("a1", "API", vec![dep("LIB", DependencyType::Required)])]);
        c.add("LIB", "Lib", "mod", vec![fabric_mod("l1", "LIB", vec![])]);
        c.add("PACKED", "Packed", "mod", vec![fabric_mod("p1", "PACKED", vec![])]);
        c.add("OPT", "Optional", "mod", vec![fabric_mod("o1", "OPT", vec![])]);
        let plan = plan(&c, &request("sodium"), Some(&fabric()), &installed(&[("PACKED", true, &[]), ("LIB", false, &[])]))
            .await
            .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(titles(&plan), vec!["Sodium", "Fabric API"]);
        assert_eq!(plan.items[0].reason, ItemReason::Requested);
        assert_eq!(plan.items[1].reason, ItemReason::Dependency { of: "Sodium".into() });
        assert_eq!(plan.items[0].file.file_name, "SOD-s1.jar");
        assert_eq!(
            plan.skipped,
            vec![
                PlanSkip { project_id: "PACKED".into(), title: "Packed".into(), reason: SkipReason::InPack },
                PlanSkip { project_id: "LIB".into(), title: "Lib".into(), reason: SkipReason::Installed },
            ]
        );
        assert_eq!(plan.new_instance, None);
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn a_diamond_and_a_cycle_plan_each_project_once() {
        let mut c = FakeCatalog::default();
        c.add("A", "A", "mod", vec![fabric_mod("a", "A", vec![dep("B", DependencyType::Required), dep("C", DependencyType::Required)])]);
        c.add("B", "B", "mod", vec![fabric_mod("b", "B", vec![dep("D", DependencyType::Required), dep("A", DependencyType::Required)])]);
        c.add("C", "C", "mod", vec![fabric_mod("c", "C", vec![dep("D", DependencyType::Required)])]);
        c.add("D", "D", "mod", vec![fabric_mod("d", "D", vec![])]);
        let plan = plan(&c, &request("A"), Some(&fabric()), &Installed::default()).await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(titles(&plan), vec!["A", "B", "C", "D"]);
        assert!(plan.skipped.is_empty());
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn dependencies_pinned_to_an_unfit_version_take_a_fitting_one_or_fail() {
        let mut c = FakeCatalog::default();
        let pinned = ApiDependency {
            version_id: Some("old".into()),
            project_id: None,
            dependency_type: DependencyType::Required,
        };
        c.add("M", "Mod", "mod", vec![fabric_mod("m", "M", vec![pinned])]);
        c.add("L", "Lib", "mod", vec![
            ver("old", "L", &["1.19.2"], &["fabric"], ".jar", vec![]),
            fabric_mod("good", "L", vec![]),
        ]);
        let ok = plan(&c, &request("M"), Some(&fabric()), &Installed::default()).await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(ok.items[1].version_id, "good");

        c.add("N", "Needy", "mod", vec![fabric_mod("n", "N", vec![dep("F", DependencyType::Required)])]);
        c.add("F", "Forge Thing", "mod", vec![ver("f", "F", &["1.20.1"], &["forge"], ".jar", vec![])]);
        let missing = plan(&c, &request("N"), Some(&fabric()), &Installed::default()).await.unwrap_err();
        assert_eq!(missing.code, ErrorCode::ContentDependencyMissing);
        assert_eq!(missing.params["name"], "Forge Thing");
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn chains_deeper_than_the_cap_fail() {
        let mut c = FakeCatalog::default();
        // P0 -> P1 -> ... -> P9: P9 sits at depth 9.
        for i in 0..10 {
            let deps = if i < 9 { vec![dep(&format!("P{}", i + 1), DependencyType::Required)] } else { vec![] };
            c.add(&format!("P{i}"), &format!("Part {i}"), "mod", vec![fabric_mod(&format!("v{i}"), &format!("P{i}"), deps)]);
        }
        let error = plan(&c, &request("P0"), Some(&fabric()), &Installed::default()).await.unwrap_err();
        assert_eq!(error.code, ErrorCode::ContentDependencyMissing);
        assert_eq!(error.params["name"], "Part 9");
        let ok = plan(&c, &request("P1"), Some(&fabric()), &Installed::default()).await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(ok.items.len(), 9);
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn conflicts_are_refused_in_both_directions() {
        let mut c = FakeCatalog::default();
        c.add("OPTI", "OptiFabric", "mod", vec![fabric_mod("o", "OPTI", vec![dep("SOD", DependencyType::Incompatible)])]);
        c.add("SOD", "Sodium", "mod", vec![fabric_mod("s", "SOD", vec![])]);
        c.add("NEW", "New Thing", "mod", vec![fabric_mod("n", "NEW", vec![])]);
        let mine = plan(&c, &request("OPTI"), Some(&fabric()), &installed(&[("SOD", false, &[])])).await.unwrap_err();
        assert_eq!(mine.code, ErrorCode::ContentConflict);
        assert_eq!(mine.params["name"], "Sodium");
        let theirs = plan(&c, &request("NEW"), Some(&fabric()), &installed(&[("SOD", false, &["NEW"])])).await.unwrap_err();
        assert_eq!(theirs.code, ErrorCode::ContentConflict);
        assert_eq!(theirs.params["name"], "Sodium");
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn shaders_bring_their_shader_mod_and_need_a_loader() {
        let mut c = FakeCatalog::default();
        c.add("SH", "Complementary", "shader", vec![ver("s", "SH", &["1.20.1"], &["iris"], ".zip", vec![])]);
        c.add("IRIS", "Iris", "mod", vec![fabric_mod("i", "IRIS", vec![dep("SOD", DependencyType::Required)])]);
        c.projects.insert("iris".into(), c.projects["IRIS"].clone());
        c.add("SOD", "Sodium", "mod", vec![fabric_mod("s1", "SOD", vec![])]);
        c.add("OCU", "Oculus", "mod", vec![ver("o", "OCU", &["1.20.1"], &["forge"], ".jar", vec![])]);
        c.projects.insert("oculus".into(), c.projects["OCU"].clone());
        let on_fabric = plan(&c, &request("SH"), Some(&fabric()), &Installed::default()).await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(titles(&on_fabric), vec!["Complementary", "Iris", "Sodium"]);
        assert_eq!(on_fabric.items[1].reason, ItemReason::ShaderMod);
        assert_eq!(on_fabric.items[0].file.file_name, "SH-s.zip");
        let forge = Target { minecraft: "1.20.1".into(), loader: Some(LoaderKind::Forge) };
        assert_eq!(titles(&plan(&c, &request("SH"), Some(&forge), &Installed::default()).await.unwrap()), vec!["Complementary", "Oculus"]);
        let vanilla = Target { minecraft: "1.20.1".into(), loader: None };
        assert_eq!(
            plan(&c, &request("SH"), Some(&vanilla), &Installed::default()).await.unwrap_err().code,
            ErrorCode::ContentNeedsLoader
        );
        assert_eq!(
            plan(&c, &request("SOD"), Some(&vanilla), &Installed::default()).await.unwrap_err().code,
            ErrorCode::ContentNeedsLoader
        );
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn a_new_instance_takes_the_newest_release_and_loader() {
        let mut c = FakeCatalog::default();
        c.add("SOD", "Sodium".repeat(20).as_str(), "mod", vec![
            ver("s", "SOD", &["1.20.1", "1.21.1", "24w14a"], &["quilt", "fabric"], ".jar", vec![dep("API", DependencyType::Required)]),
        ]);
        c.add("API", "Fabric API", "mod", vec![
            ver("a-old", "API", &["1.20.1"], &["fabric"], ".jar", vec![]),
            ver("a-new", "API", &["1.21.1"], &["fabric"], ".jar", vec![]),
        ]);
        let p = plan(&c, &request("SOD"), None, &Installed::default()).await.unwrap();
        let new = p.new_instance.clone().unwrap();
        assert_eq!((new.minecraft.as_str(), new.loader), ("1.21.1", Some(LoaderKind::Fabric)));
        assert_eq!(new.name.chars().count(), 64);
        assert_eq!(p.target, Target { minecraft: "1.21.1".into(), loader: Some(LoaderKind::Fabric) });
        assert_eq!(p.items[1].version_id, "a-new", "dependencies resolve against the new instance");

        c.add("RP", "Faithful", "resourcepack", vec![ver("r", "RP", &["1.21.1"], &["minecraft"], ".zip", vec![])]);
        let rp = plan(&c, &request("RP"), None, &Installed::default()).await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(rp.new_instance.unwrap().loader, None);
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn refusals_for_installed_modpacks_and_wrong_versions() {
        let mut c = FakeCatalog::default();
        c.add("SOD", "Sodium", "mod", vec![fabric_mod("s", "SOD", vec![])]);
        c.add("OTHER", "Other", "mod", vec![fabric_mod("x", "OTHER", vec![])]);
        c.add("PACK", "Pack", "modpack", vec![ver("p", "PACK", &["1.20.1"], &["fabric"], ".mrpack", vec![])]);
        let again = plan(&c, &request("SOD"), Some(&fabric()), &installed(&[("SOD", false, &[])])).await.unwrap_err();
        assert_eq!(again.code, ErrorCode::ContentAlreadyInstalled);
        assert_eq!(again.params["name"], "Sodium");
        assert_eq!(
            plan(&c, &request("PACK"), Some(&fabric()), &Installed::default()).await.unwrap_err().code,
            ErrorCode::ContentIncompatible
        );
        let foreign = PlanRequest { project: "SOD".into(), version: Some("x".into()) };
        assert_eq!(
            plan(&c, &foreign, Some(&fabric()), &Installed::default()).await.unwrap_err().code,
            ErrorCode::ContentIncompatible
        );
        let old_game = Target { minecraft: "1.16.5".into(), loader: Some(LoaderKind::Fabric) };
        let none = plan(&c, &request("SOD"), Some(&old_game), &Installed::default()).await.unwrap_err();
        assert_eq!(none.code, ErrorCode::ContentIncompatible);
        assert_eq!(none.params["name"], "Sodium");
        let mut server_only = c.projects["SOD"].clone();
        server_only.client_side = "unsupported".into();
        c.projects.insert("SOD".into(), server_only);
        assert_eq!(
            plan(&c, &request("SOD"), Some(&fabric()), &Installed::default()).await.unwrap_err().code,
            ErrorCode::ContentIncompatible
        );
    }
}
