```rust
//! What installing a project means (spec §3.4): the version that fits, its required
//! dependencies (and a shader mod for shaders), what's already there, and what can't work.
use std::{
    collections::{HashSet, VecDeque},
    future::Future,
};

use serde::Serialize;

use super::{
    api::{not_found, Api, ApiProject, ApiVersion, DependencyType},
    compat::{file_hashes, fits, new_target, pick, shader_mod, usable_file, ContentKind, Target},
    identify::Installed,
    project::fetch_versions,
};
use crate::{
    download::Hashes,
    error::{ErrorCode, LauncherError, Result},
    instances::MAX_NAME_CHARS,
    meta::loaders::LoaderKind,
};

pub const MAX_DEPTH: usize = 8;
pub const MAX_ITEMS: usize = 50;

/// Where the planner looks things up. `Api` in the app; a fake in the tests.
pub trait Catalog: Send + Sync {
    fn project<'a>(&'a self, id: &'a str) -> impl Future<Output = Result<ApiProject>> + Send + 'a;
    fn versions<'a>(
        &'a self,
        project_id: &'a str,
        target: Option<&'a Target>,
        kind: ContentKind,
    ) -> impl Future<Output = Result<Vec<ApiVersion>>> + Send + 'a;
    fn version<'a>(&'a self, id: &'a str) -> impl Future<Output = Result<ApiVersion>> + Send + 'a;
}

impl Catalog for Api {
    fn project<'a>(&'a self, id: &'a str) -> impl Future<Output = Result<ApiProject>> + Send + 'a {
        Api::project(self, id)
    }
    fn versions<'a>(
        &'a self,
        project_id: &'a str,
        target: Option<&'a Target>,
        kind: ContentKind,
    ) -> impl Future<Output = Result<Vec<ApiVersion>>> + Send + 'a {
        fetch_versions(self, project_id, kind, target)
    }
    fn version<'a>(&'a self, id: &'a str) -> impl Future<Output = Result<ApiVersion>> + Send + 'a {
        Api::version(self, id)
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct PlanRequest {
    pub project: String,
    pub version: Option<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
#[serde(tag = "kind", rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum ItemReason {
    Requested,
    Dependency { of: String },
    ShaderMod,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum SkipReason {
    Installed,
    InPack,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct PlanSkip {
    pub project_id: String,
    pub title: String,
    pub reason: SkipReason,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct PlannedFile {
    pub url: String,
    pub file_name: String,
    pub size: u64,
    pub hashes: Hashes,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct PlanItem {
    pub project_id: String,
    pub title: String,
    pub kind: ContentKind,
    pub version_id: String,
    pub version_number: String,
    pub reason: ItemReason,
    pub file: PlannedFile,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct NewInstancePlan {
    pub name: String,
    pub minecraft: String,
    pub loader: Option<LoaderKind>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct InstallPlan {
    /// What everything was resolved against (the new instance's game for `new_instance`).
    pub target: Target,
    pub new_instance: Option<NewInstancePlan>,
    /// The requested project first, then dependencies in the order they were resolved.
    pub items: Vec<PlanItem>,
    pub skipped: Vec<PlanSkip>,
}

fn incompatible(title: &str) -> LauncherError {
    LauncherError::pack(
        ErrorCode::ContentIncompatible,
        format!("{title} doesn't have a version for this instance"),
    )
    .with_param("name", title)
}

fn needs_loader() -> LauncherError {
    LauncherError::pack(
        ErrorCode::ContentNeedsLoader,
        "This needs a mod loader. Pick an instance with Fabric, Quilt, Forge or NeoForge",
    )
}

fn dependency_missing(name: &str) -> LauncherError {
    LauncherError::pack(
        ErrorCode::ContentDependencyMissing,
        format!("It needs {name}, which has no version for this instance"),
    )
    .with_param("name", name)
}

fn conflict(name: &str) -> LauncherError {
    LauncherError::pack(
        ErrorCode::ContentConflict,
        format!("It doesn't work together with {name}"),
    )
    .with_param("name", name)
}

fn already_installed(name: &str) -> LauncherError {
    LauncherError::pack(
        ErrorCode::ContentAlreadyInstalled,
        format!("{name} is already in this instance"),
    )
    .with_param("name", name)
}

fn item(project: &ApiProject, kind: ContentKind, v: &ApiVersion, reason: ItemReason) -> Result<PlanItem> {
    let file = usable_file(v, kind).ok_or_else(|| incompatible(&project.title))?;
    Ok(PlanItem {
        project_id: project.id.clone(),
        title: project.title.clone(),
        kind,
        version_id: v.id.clone(),
        version_number: v.version_number.clone(),
        reason,
        file: PlannedFile {
            url: file.url.clone(),
            file_name: file.filename.clone(),
            size: file.size,
            hashes: file_hashes(file),
        },
    })
}

/// A dependency waiting to be resolved: by project (id or slug), by version, or both.
struct Pending {
    project: Option<String>,
    version: Option<String>,
    reason: ItemReason,
    depth: usize,
}

struct Planner<'a, C: Catalog> {
    catalog: &'a C,
    target: &'a Target,
    installed: &'a Installed,
    items: Vec<PlanItem>,
    skipped: Vec<PlanSkip>,
    seen: HashSet<String>,
}

impl<C: Catalog> Planner<'_, C> {
    fn enqueue_deps(&self, queue: &mut VecDeque<Pending>, v: &ApiVersion, title: &str, depth: usize) {
        for d in v
            .dependencies
            .iter()
            .filter(|d| d.dependency_type == DependencyType::Required)
        {
            queue.push_back(Pending {
                project: d.project_id.clone(),
                version: d.version_id.clone(),
                reason: ItemReason::Dependency { of: title.to_string() },
                depth,
            });
        }
    }

    /// A planned version that can't live with something installed, or the other way round.
    async fn check_conflicts(&self, v: &ApiVersion) -> Result<()> {
        let clash = v
            .dependencies
            .iter()
            .filter(|d| d.dependency_type == DependencyType::Incompatible)
            .filter_map(|d| d.project_id.as_ref())
            .find(|p| self.installed.projects.contains_key(*p))
            .cloned()
            .or_else(|| {
                self.installed
                    .projects
                    .iter()
                    .find(|(_, i)| i.incompatible.contains(&v.project_id))
                    .map(|(id, _)| id.clone())
            });
        match clash {
            None => Ok(()),
            Some(id) => {
                let name = self.catalog.project(&id).await.map(|p| p.title).unwrap_or(id);
                Err(conflict(&name))
            }
        }
    }

    async fn add(&mut self, next: Pending, queue: &mut VecDeque<Pending>) -> Result<()> {
        if next.project.as_ref().is_some_and(|p| self.seen.contains(p)) {
            return Ok(());
        }
        let pinned = match &next.version {
            Some(id) => Some(self.catalog.version(id).await?),
            None => None,
        };
        let project_ref = match (&next.project, &pinned) {
            (Some(p), _) => p.clone(),
            (None, Some(v)) => v.project_id.clone(),
            (None, None) => return Ok(()),
        };
        let project = self.catalog.project(&project_ref).await?;
        if !self.seen.insert(project.id.clone()) {
            return Ok(());
        }
        if let Some(found) = self.installed.projects.get(&project.id) {
            self.skipped.push(PlanSkip {
                project_id: project.id.clone(),
                title: project.title.clone(),
                reason: if found.managed {
                    SkipReason::InPack
                } else {
                    SkipReason::Installed
                },
            });
            return Ok(());
        }
        if next.depth > MAX_DEPTH || self.items.len() >= MAX_ITEMS {
            return Err(dependency_missing(&project.title));
        }
        let kind = match ContentKind::from_project_type(&project.project_type) {
            Some(ContentKind::Modpack) | None => return Err(dependency_missing(&project.title)),
            Some(kind) => kind,
        };
        let version = match pinned {
            Some(v) if v.project_id == project.id && fits(&v, kind, self.target) => v,
            _ => {
                let list = self
                    .catalog
                    .versions(&project.id, Some(self.target), kind)
                    .await?;
                pick(&list, |v| fits(v, kind, self.target))
                    .cloned()
                    .ok_or_else(|| dependency_missing(&project.title))?
            }
        };
        self.check_conflicts(&version).await?;
        self.items.push(item(&project, kind, &version, next.reason)?);
        self.enqueue_deps(queue, &version, &project.title, next.depth + 1);
        Ok(())
    }
}

/// Plan installing `request` into `target` (an instance), or into a new instance when `target`
/// is `None`. `installed` is what the instance already has (empty for a new one).
pub async fn plan<C: Catalog>(
    catalog: &C,
    request: &PlanRequest,
    target: Option<&Target>,
    installed: &Installed,
) -> Result<InstallPlan> {
    let project = catalog.project(&request.project).await?;
    let kind = match ContentKind::from_project_type(&project.project_type) {
        Some(kind) if kind != ContentKind::Modpack && project.client_side != "unsupported" => kind,
        _ => return Err(incompatible(&project.title)),
    };
    if target.is_some_and(|t| t.loader.is_none())
        && matches!(kind, ContentKind::Mod | ContentKind::Shader)
    {
        return Err(needs_loader());
    }
    let pinned = match &request.version {
        Some(id) => {
            let v = catalog.version(id).await?;
            if v.project_id != project.id {
                return Err(incompatible(&project.title));
            }
            Some(v)
        }
        None => None,
    };
    let (version, target, new_instance) = match target {
        Some(t) => {
            let v = match pinned {
                Some(v) if fits(&v, kind, t) => v,
                Some(_) => return Err(incompatible(&project.title)),
                None => {
                    let list = catalog.versions(&project.id, Some(t), kind).await?;
                    pick(&list, |v| fits(v, kind, t))
                        .cloned()
                        .ok_or_else(|| incompatible(&project.title))?
                }
            };
            (v, t.clone(), None)
        }
        None => {
            let v = match pinned {
                Some(v) => v,
                None => {
                    let list = catalog.versions(&project.id, None, kind).await?;
                    pick(&list, |v| new_target(v, kind).is_some())
                        .cloned()
                        .ok_or_else(|| incompatible(&project.title))?
                }
            };
            let t = new_target(&v, kind).ok_or_else(|| incompatible(&project.title))?;
            let name: String = project.title.trim().chars().take(MAX_NAME_CHARS).collect();
            let new = NewInstancePlan {
                name: if name.trim().is_empty() { project.slug.clone() } else { name.trim().to_string() },
                minecraft: t.minecraft.clone(),
                loader: t.loader,
            };
            (v, t, Some(new))
        }
    };
    if installed.projects.contains_key(&project.id) {
        return Err(already_installed(&project.title));
    }
    let mut planner = Planner {
        catalog,
        target: &target,
        installed,
        items: Vec::new(),
        skipped: Vec::new(),
        seen: HashSet::from([project.id.clone()]),
    };
    planner.check_conflicts(&version).await?;
    planner
        .items
        .push(item(&project, kind, &version, ItemReason::Requested)?);
    let mut queue = VecDeque::new();
    planner.enqueue_deps(&mut queue, &version, &project.title, 1);
    if kind == ContentKind::Shader {
        let slug = shader_mod(target.loader).ok_or_else(needs_loader)?;
        queue.push_back(Pending {
            project: Some(slug.to_string()),
            version: None,
            reason: ItemReason::ShaderMod,
            depth: 1,
        });
    }
    while let Some(next) = queue.pop_front() {
        planner.add(next, &mut queue).await?;
    }
    Ok(InstallPlan {
        target: target.clone(),
        new_instance,
        items: planner.items,
        skipped: planner.skipped,
    })
}
```

Note: the shader mod's `Pending.project` is a slug (`iris`), so the early `seen` check by id doesn't catch it; the check after `catalog.project` does.

```rust
use std::{
    sync::{Arc, Mutex},
    time::Duration,
};

use launcher_core::{
    browse::{
        compat::ContentKind as Kind,
        install::install,
        plan::{ItemReason, PlanItem, PlannedFile},
    },
    download::{cache::ObjectCache, Downloader},
    events::{Event, EventSink, TaskId},
};

fn downloader() -> Downloader {
    Downloader::new(launcher_core::http::client("test").unwrap()).with_backoff(Duration::from_millis(1))
}

async fn host_file(server: &MockServer, name: &str, body: &[u8]) {
    Mock::given(method("GET"))
        .and(path(format!("/files/{name}")))
        .respond_with(ResponseTemplate::new(200).set_body_bytes(body.to_vec()))
        .mount(server)
        .await;
}

fn planned(server: &MockServer, title: &str, kind: Kind, name: &str, body: &[u8]) -> PlanItem {
    PlanItem {
        project_id: title.to_lowercase(),
        title: title.into(),
        kind,
        version_id: "v1".into(),
        version_number: "1.0".into(),
        reason: ItemReason::Requested,
        file: PlannedFile {
            url: format!("{}/files/{name}", server.uri()),
            file_name: name.into(),
            size: body.len() as u64,
            hashes: Hashes {
                sha1: Some(sha1_hex(body)),
                sha512: Some(sha512_hex(body)),
                ..Hashes::default()
            },
        },
    }
}

fn recorder() -> (EventSink, Arc<Mutex<Vec<Event>>>) {
    let events = Arc::new(Mutex::new(Vec::new()));
    let seen = events.clone();
    (Arc::new(move |e| seen.lock().unwrap().push(e)), events)
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn content_goes_into_its_folder_without_overwriting() {
    let server = MockServer::start().await;
    host_file(&server, "sodium.jar", b"sodium").await;
    host_file(&server, "faithful.zip", b"faithful").await;
    let root = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let cache = ObjectCache::new(root.path().join("objects"));
    let game = root.path().join("game");
    let items = vec![
        planned(&server, "Sodium", Kind::Mod, "sodium.jar", b"sodium"),
        planned(&server, "Faithful", Kind::ResourcePack, "faithful.zip", b"faithful"),
    ];
    let (sink, events) = recorder();
    let task = TaskId::new("install_content").with("name", "Sodium");
    let added = install(&cache, &downloader(), &game, &items, None, &task, &sink).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(added, vec!["sodium.jar".to_string(), "faithful.zip".into()]);
    assert_eq!(std::fs::read(game.join("mods/sodium.jar")).unwrap(), b"sodium");
    assert_eq!(std::fs::read(game.join("resourcepacks/faithful.zip")).unwrap(), b"faithful");
    let last = events.lock().unwrap().last().cloned().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(last, Event::Progress { task: task.clone(), done: 2, total: 2, bytes: 14 });
    assert!(std::fs::read_dir(game.join("mods")).unwrap().count() == 1, "no temp files left");

    // The same file again is skipped; a different file with that name is refused.
    assert!(install(&cache, &downloader(), &game, &items[..1], None, &task, &sink).await.unwrap().is_empty());
    std::fs::write(game.join("mods/sodium.jar"), b"player's own").unwrap();
    let exists = install(&cache, &downloader(), &game, &items[..1], None, &task, &sink).await.unwrap_err();
    assert_eq!(exists.code, ErrorCode::ModExists);
    assert_eq!(std::fs::read(game.join("mods/sodium.jar")).unwrap(), b"player's own");

    // A disabled twin counts as present.
    host_file(&server, "lithium.jar", b"lithium").await;
    std::fs::write(game.join("mods/lithium.jar.disabled"), b"old").unwrap();
    let twin = planned(&server, "Lithium", Kind::Mod, "lithium.jar", b"lithium");
    assert_eq!(
        install(&cache, &downloader(), &game, &[twin], None, &task, &sink).await.unwrap_err().code,
        ErrorCode::ModExists
    );
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn pack_paths_and_corrupt_downloads_are_refused() {
    let server = MockServer::start().await;
    host_file(&server, "jei.jar", b"tampered").await;
    let root = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let cache = ObjectCache::new(root.path().join("objects"));
    let game = root.path().join("game");
    let (sink, _) = recorder();
    let task = TaskId::new("install_content");
    let lock = managed(&[("mods/JEI.jar", b"jei")]);
    let jei = planned(&server, "JEI", Kind::Mod, "jei.jar", b"jei");
    let refused = install(&cache, &downloader(), &game, std::slice::from_ref(&jei), Some(&lock), &task, &sink)
        .await
        .unwrap_err();
    assert_eq!(refused.code, ErrorCode::ModManaged);
    assert_eq!(hits(&server, "/files/jei.jar").await, 0, "refused before downloading");
    let corrupt = install(&cache, &downloader(), &game, &[jei], None, &task, &sink).await.unwrap_err();
    assert_eq!(corrupt.code, ErrorCode::DownloadCorrupt);
    assert!(!game.join("mods/jei.jar").exists());
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn hostile_file_names_are_refused_before_downloading() {
    let server = MockServer::start().await;
    let root = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let cache = ObjectCache::new(root.path().join("objects"));
    let game = root.path().join("game");
    let (sink, _) = recorder();
    let task = TaskId::new("install_content");
    for name in ["../mods/x.jar", "CON.jar", "a/b.jar", r"a\b.jar", "x.jar.", "x.zip", ""] {
        let item = planned(&server, "X", Kind::Mod, name, b"x");
        let error = install(&cache, &downloader(), &game, &[item], None, &task, &sink).await.unwrap_err();
        assert_eq!(error.code, ErrorCode::ModNameInvalid, "{name:?}");
    }
    let shader = planned(&server, "S", Kind::Shader, "s.jar", b"s");
    assert_eq!(
        install(&cache, &downloader(), &game, &[shader], None, &task, &sink).await.unwrap_err().code,
        ErrorCode::ModNameInvalid
    );
    assert!(server.received_requests().await.unwrap().is_empty());
    assert!(!game.exists() || std::fs::read_dir(&game).unwrap().next().is_none());
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

Create `crates/launcher-core/src/browse/install.rs`:

```rust
//! Writing planned content into the game folder (spec §3.6). Every file is verified in the
//! object cache first, then created next to its final name and linked in without replacing
//! anything: a player's file is never overwritten.
use std::{io::ErrorKind, path::Path};

use super::plan::PlanItem;
use crate::{
    download::{cache::ObjectCache, hash::sha1_file, Downloader},
    error::{ErrorCode, LauncherError, Result},
    events::{Event, EventSink, TaskId},
    fsutil::{path_key, safe_component},
    sync::lockfile::Managed,
};

fn name_invalid(name: &str) -> LauncherError {
    LauncherError::disk(ErrorCode::ModNameInvalid, "That isn't a mod file name").with_detail(name)
}

fn exists(name: &str) -> LauncherError {
    LauncherError::disk(
        ErrorCode::ModExists,
        format!("{name} is already in this instance"),
    )
    .with_param("file", name)
}

fn managed_path(name: &str) -> LauncherError {
    LauncherError::disk(ErrorCode::ModManaged, format!("{name} comes with the pack"))
        .with_param("file", name)
}

fn join_error(e: tokio::task::JoinError) -> LauncherError {
    std::io::Error::other(e).into()
}

/// The file's path relative to the game folder, if its name is one safe component with the
/// kind's extension.
fn relative_path(item: &PlanItem) -> Result<String> {
    let name = &item.file.file_name;
    let folder = item.kind.folder().ok_or_else(|| name_invalid(name))?;
    if !safe_component(name) || !name.to_ascii_lowercase().ends_with(item.kind.extension()) {
        return Err(name_invalid(name));
    }
    Ok(format!("{folder}/{name}"))
}

/// Move `tmp` to `dest` unless something is already at `dest`. A hard link fails atomically
/// when the name is taken; file systems without hard links fall back to check-then-rename.
fn link_no_replace(tmp: &Path, dest: &Path) -> std::io::Result<()> {
    let result = match std::fs::hard_link(tmp, dest) {
        Ok(()) => Ok(()),
        Err(e) if e.kind() == ErrorKind::AlreadyExists => Err(e),
        Err(_) if dest.exists() => Err(ErrorKind::AlreadyExists.into()),
        Err(_) => return std::fs::rename(tmp, dest),
    };
    let _ = std::fs::remove_file(tmp);
    result
}

async fn same_file(path: &Path, sha1: Option<&str>) -> Result<bool> {
    let (Some(expected), owned) = (sha1, path.to_path_buf()) else {
        return Ok(false);
    };
    let actual = tokio::task::spawn_blocking(move || sha1_file(&owned))
        .await
        .map_err(join_error)??;
    Ok(actual.eq_ignore_ascii_case(expected))
}

pub async fn install(
    cache: &ObjectCache,
    downloader: &Downloader,
    game_dir: &Path,
    items: &[PlanItem],
    managed: Option<&Managed>,
    task: &TaskId,
    sink: &EventSink,
) -> Result<Vec<String>> {
    // Refuse bad names and pack-owned paths before anything is downloaded.
    let mut paths = Vec::with_capacity(items.len());
    for item in items {
        let rel = relative_path(item)?;
        let key = path_key(&rel);
        if managed.is_some_and(|m| m.files.keys().any(|k| path_key(k) == key)) {
            return Err(managed_path(&item.file.file_name));
        }
        paths.push(rel);
    }
    let total = items.len() as u64;
    let (mut done, mut bytes) = (0u64, 0u64);
    let progress = |done, bytes| {
        sink(Event::Progress {
            task: task.clone(),
            done,
            total,
            bytes,
        })
    };
    progress(done, bytes);
    let mut added = Vec::new();
    for (item, rel) in items.iter().zip(&paths) {
        let name = &item.file.file_name;
        let dest = game_dir.join(rel);
        let mut disabled = dest.as_os_str().to_owned();
        disabled.push(".disabled");
        if tokio::fs::try_exists(&dest).await? {
            if !same_file(&dest, item.file.hashes.sha1.as_deref()).await? {
                return Err(exists(name));
            }
        } else if tokio::fs::try_exists(&disabled).await? {
            return Err(exists(name));
        } else {
            let object = cache
                .ensure(
                    downloader,
                    std::slice::from_ref(&item.file.url),
                    &item.file.hashes,
                    Some(item.file.size),
                )
                .await?;
            let dir = dest.parent().expect("a folder and a name");
            tokio::fs::create_dir_all(dir).await?;
            let tmp = dir.join(format!(".{name}.{}.perland-tmp", std::process::id()));
            tokio::fs::copy(&object, &tmp).await?;
            let (from, to) = (tmp.clone(), dest.clone());
            match tokio::task::spawn_blocking(move || link_no_replace(&from, &to))
                .await
                .map_err(join_error)?
            {
                Ok(()) => added.push(name.clone()),
                Err(e) if e.kind() == ErrorKind::AlreadyExists => return Err(exists(name)),
                Err(e) => {
                    let _ = tokio::fs::remove_file(&tmp).await;
                    return Err(e.into());
                }
            }
        }
        done += 1;
        bytes += item.file.size;
        progress(done, bytes);
    }
    Ok(added)
}
```

```rust
use launcher_core::browse::images::{ImageCache, ImageKind};

const PNG: &[u8] = b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n\0\0\0\rIHDR";

#[tokio::test]
async fn images_come_from_the_cdn_once_and_only_when_real() {
    let server = MockServer::start().await;
    host_file(&server, "icon.png", PNG).await;
    host_file(&server, "page.html", b"<html>login</html>").await;
    host_file(&server, "anim.gif", b"GIF89a\x01\x00\x01\x00").await;
    host_file(&server, "big.png", &[PNG, &[0u8; 64]].concat()).await;
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let cache = ImageCache::new(launcher_core::http::client("test").unwrap(), dir.path(), &server.uri())
        .with_limits(32, 1 << 20, 1 << 20);
    let url = |name: &str| format!("{}/files/{name}", server.uri());

    let icon = cache.get(&url("icon.png"), ImageKind::Icon).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!((icon.bytes.as_slice(), icon.mime), (PNG, "image/png"));
    assert_eq!(cache.get(&url("anim.gif"), ImageKind::Icon).await.unwrap().mime, "image/gif");
    for refused in ["page.html", "big.png"] {
        assert_eq!(
            cache.get(&url(refused), ImageKind::Icon).await.unwrap_err().code,
            ErrorCode::ImageRefused,
            "{refused}"
        );
    }
    assert_eq!(cache.get(&url("big.png"), ImageKind::Gallery).await.unwrap().bytes.len(), 80);
    assert_eq!(
        cache.get("https://i.imgur.com/x.png", ImageKind::Icon).await.unwrap_err().code,
        ErrorCode::ImageRefused
    );

    // Offline, a cached image still comes back.
    server.reset().await;
    assert_eq!(cache.get(&url("icon.png"), ImageKind::Icon).await.unwrap().bytes, PNG);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn the_image_cache_prunes_the_oldest_first() {
    let server = MockServer::start().await;
    for name in ["a.png", "b.png", "c.png"] {
        host_file(&server, name, &[PNG, &[0u8; 40]].concat()).await;
    }
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let cache = ImageCache::new(launcher_core::http::client("test").unwrap(), dir.path(), &server.uri())
        .with_limits(1 << 20, 1 << 20, 120);
    let url = |name: &str| format!("{}/files/{name}", server.uri());
    cache.get(&url("a.png"), ImageKind::Icon).await.unwrap();
    // Make "a" clearly the oldest, whatever the file system's timestamp resolution.
    for entry in std::fs::read_dir(dir.path()).unwrap() {
        let file = std::fs::File::options().write(true).open(entry.unwrap().path()).unwrap();
        file.set_modified(std::time::SystemTime::UNIX_EPOCH).unwrap();
    }
    cache.get(&url("b.png"), ImageKind::Icon).await.unwrap();
    cache.get(&url("c.png"), ImageKind::Icon).await.unwrap();
    let total: u64 = std::fs::read_dir(dir.path())
        .unwrap()
        .map(|e| e.unwrap().metadata().unwrap().len())
        .sum();
    assert!(total <= 120, "{total}");
    server.reset().await;
    assert!(cache.get(&url("a.png"), ImageKind::Icon).await.is_err(), "a was pruned");
    assert!(cache.get(&url("c.png"), ImageKind::Icon).await.is_ok(), "c is the newest");
}
```

Above the tests in `images.rs`:

```rust
//! Project icons and gallery images for Browse (spec §3.7). The app's CSP allows no remote
//! images, so the core fetches them from Modrinth's CDN only, under a size cap, keeps only real
//! images, and caches them on disk, pruned oldest first.
use std::{
    path::{Path, PathBuf},
    sync::Arc,
    time::SystemTime,
};

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use tokio::sync::Semaphore;

use crate::{
    download::hash::sha256_hex,
    error::{ErrorCode, LauncherError, Result},
    fsutil::write_atomic,
    sources::art::sniff,
};

pub const MAX_ICON_BYTES: usize = 1 << 20;
pub const MAX_GALLERY_BYTES: usize = 5 << 20;
pub const MAX_CACHE_BYTES: u64 = 100 << 20;
const AT_ONCE: usize = 6;
const EXTENSIONS: [&str; 5] = ["png", "jpg", "webp", "gif", "svg"];

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum ImageKind {
    Icon,
    Gallery,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Image {
    pub bytes: Vec<u8>,
    pub mime: &'static str,
}

/// The 3b pack-art sniffer, plus GIF (many mod icons are animated).
pub fn sniff_image(bytes: &[u8]) -> Option<&'static str> {
    if bytes.starts_with(b"GIF87a") || bytes.starts_with(b"GIF89a") {
        return Some("gif");
    }
    sniff(bytes)
}

fn mime(ext: &str) -> &'static str {
    match ext {
        "png" => "image/png",
        "jpg" => "image/jpeg",
        "webp" => "image/webp",
        "gif" => "image/gif",
        _ => "image/svg+xml",
    }
}

fn refused(detail: impl std::fmt::Display) -> LauncherError {
    LauncherError::network(ErrorCode::ImageRefused, "That image can't be shown").with_detail(detail)
}

pub struct ImageCache {
    http: reqwest::Client,
    dir: PathBuf,
    origin: Option<reqwest::Url>,
    icon_limit: usize,
    gallery_limit: usize,
    total_limit: u64,
    permits: Arc<Semaphore>,
}

impl ImageCache {
    /// Images only from `origin` (`https://cdn.modrinth.com`; a wiremock server in tests).
    pub fn new(http: reqwest::Client, dir: impl Into<PathBuf>, origin: &str) -> Self {
        Self {
            http,
            dir: dir.into(),
            origin: reqwest::Url::parse(origin).ok(),
            icon_limit: MAX_ICON_BYTES,
            gallery_limit: MAX_GALLERY_BYTES,
            total_limit: MAX_CACHE_BYTES,
            permits: Arc::new(Semaphore::new(AT_ONCE)),
        }
    }

    pub fn with_limits(mut self, icon: usize, gallery: usize, total: u64) -> Self {
        self.icon_limit = icon;
        self.gallery_limit = gallery;
        self.total_limit = total;
        self
    }

    /// Same scheme, host and port as the origin, and no user info.
    pub fn allows(&self, raw: &str) -> bool {
        let (Some(origin), Ok(url)) = (&self.origin, reqwest::Url::parse(raw)) else {
            return false;
        };
        url.scheme() == origin.scheme()
            && url.host_str() == origin.host_str()
            && url.port_or_known_default() == origin.port_or_known_default()
            && url.username().is_empty()
            && url.password().is_none()
    }

    fn limit(&self, kind: ImageKind) -> usize {
        match kind {
            ImageKind::Icon => self.icon_limit,
            ImageKind::Gallery => self.gallery_limit,
        }
    }

    pub async fn get(&self, raw: &str, kind: ImageKind) -> Result<Image> {
        if !self.allows(raw) {
            return Err(refused("not from the image server"));
        }
        let limit = self.limit(kind);
        let stem = sha256_hex(raw.as_bytes());
        for ext in EXTENSIONS {
            let path = self.dir.join(format!("{stem}.{ext}"));
            if let Ok(bytes) = tokio::fs::read(&path).await {
                if bytes.len() <= limit {
                    return Ok(Image {
                        bytes,
                        mime: mime(ext),
                    });
                }
            }
        }
        let _permit = self.permits.acquire().await.map_err(refused)?;
        let bytes = self.download(raw, limit).await?;
        let ext = sniff_image(&bytes).ok_or_else(|| refused("not an image"))?;
        if write_atomic(&self.dir.join(format!("{stem}.{ext}")), &bytes)
            .await
            .is_ok()
        {
            self.prune().await;
        }
        Ok(Image {
            bytes,
            mime: mime(ext),
        })
    }

    async fn download(&self, raw: &str, limit: usize) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
        let mut response = self.http.get(raw).send().await.map_err(|e| refused(e.without_url()))?;
        if !response.status().is_success() {
            return Err(refused(response.status()));
        }
        if response.content_length().is_some_and(|n| n > limit as u64) {
            return Err(refused("too big"));
        }
        let mut body = Vec::new();
        while let Some(chunk) = response.chunk().await.map_err(|e| refused(e.without_url()))? {
            body.extend_from_slice(&chunk);
            if body.len() > limit {
                return Err(refused("too big"));
            }
        }
        Ok(body)
    }

    /// Delete the least recently written images until the folder fits the cap. Best effort.
    async fn prune(&self) {
        let Ok(mut entries) = tokio::fs::read_dir(&self.dir).await else {
            return;
        };
        let mut files: Vec<(SystemTime, u64, PathBuf)> = Vec::new();
        while let Ok(Some(entry)) = entries.next_entry().await {
            if let Ok(meta) = entry.metadata().await {
                if meta.is_file() {
                    let when = meta.modified().unwrap_or(SystemTime::UNIX_EPOCH);
                    files.push((when, meta.len(), entry.path()));
                }
            }
        }
        let mut total: u64 = files.iter().map(|(_, len, _)| len).sum();
        files.sort_by_key(|(when, _, _)| *when);
        for (_, len, path) in files {
            if total <= self.total_limit {
                break;
            }
            if remove(&path).await {
                total -= len;
            }
        }
    }
}

async fn remove(path: &Path) -> bool {
    tokio::fs::remove_file(path).await.is_ok()
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the service**

Replace `crates/launcher-core/src/browse/mod.rs` with:

```rust
//! Browse (Plan 3c): Modrinth search, projects, and installing mods, resource packs and shaders
//! into an instance with their required dependencies. Modpacks go through `sources` instead.
pub mod api;
pub mod compat;
pub mod identify;
pub mod images;
pub mod install;
pub mod plan;
pub mod project;
pub mod search;

use std::{collections::HashMap, path::Path};

use tokio::sync::Mutex;

use self::{
    api::Api,
    compat::{ContentKind, Target},
    identify::{FileHashes, Installed},
    images::{Image, ImageCache, ImageKind},
    plan::{InstallPlan, PlanItem, PlanRequest},
    project::{ProjectDetail, VersionInfo},
    search::{SearchPage, SearchQuery},
};
use crate::{
    download::{cache::ObjectCache, Downloader},
    error::Result,
    events::{EventSink, TaskId},
    paths::LauncherDirs,
    sync::lockfile::Managed,
};

/// Upstream addresses, overridable for tests.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct BrowseConfig {
    pub api: String,
    pub cdn: String,
}

impl Default for BrowseConfig {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            api: "https://api.modrinth.com/v2".into(),
            cdn: "https://cdn.modrinth.com".into(),
        }
    }
}

/// Everything Browse does, for the app. One per process: it remembers file hashes and
/// categories between calls.
pub struct Browse {
    pub api: Api,
    images: ImageCache,
    cache: ObjectCache,
    downloader: Downloader,
    hashes: Mutex<FileHashes>,
    categories: Mutex<HashMap<ContentKind, Vec<String>>>,
}

impl Browse {
    pub fn new(
        http: reqwest::Client,
        downloader: Downloader,
        dirs: &LauncherDirs,
        config: BrowseConfig,
    ) -> Self {
        Self {
            api: Api::new(http.clone(), config.api),
            images: ImageCache::new(http, dirs.images(), &config.cdn),
            cache: ObjectCache::new(dirs.objects()),
            downloader,
            hashes: Mutex::new(FileHashes::default()),
            categories: Mutex::new(HashMap::new()),
        }
    }

    pub async fn search(&self, query: &SearchQuery) -> Result<SearchPage> {
        search::search(&self.api, query).await
    }

    pub async fn categories(&self, kind: ContentKind) -> Result<Vec<String>> {
        if let Some(known) = self.categories.lock().await.get(&kind) {
            return Ok(known.clone());
        }
        let fresh = search::categories(&self.api, kind).await?;
        self.categories.lock().await.insert(kind, fresh.clone());
        Ok(fresh)
    }

    pub async fn project(&self, id: &str) -> Result<ProjectDetail> {
        project::project(&self.api, id).await
    }

    pub async fn versions(&self, id: &str, target: Option<&Target>) -> Result<Vec<VersionInfo>> {
        project::versions(&self.api, id, target).await
    }

    pub async fn identify(&self, game_dir: &Path, managed: Option<&Managed>) -> Result<Installed> {
        let mut hashes = self.hashes.lock().await;
        identify::identify(&self.api, game_dir, managed, &mut hashes).await
    }

    pub async fn plan(
        &self,
        request: &PlanRequest,
        target: Option<&Target>,
        installed: &Installed,
    ) -> Result<InstallPlan> {
        plan::plan(&self.api, request, target, installed).await
    }

    pub async fn install(
        &self,
        game_dir: &Path,
        items: &[PlanItem],
        managed: Option<&Managed>,
        task: &TaskId,
        sink: &EventSink,
    ) -> Result<Vec<String>> {
        install::install(&self.cache, &self.downloader, game_dir, items, managed, task, sink).await
    }

    pub async fn image(&self, url: &str, kind: ImageKind) -> Result<Image> {
        self.images.get(url, kind).await
    }
}
```

### Task 10: `find_update` reuses a resolved update (3b carry-forward)

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/launcher-core/src/sync/mod.rs` (`Syncer` field, `new`, `find_update`, `sync_locked`)
- Test: `crates/launcher-core/tests/sync.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Changes no public signature. `Syncer` gains a private field `resolved: std::sync::Mutex<HashMap<String, (String, SourceRef, DesiredState)>>` (instance id → offered stamp, source, resolved pack).

Spec §5 says the memo is keyed by (instance id, installed stamp, offered stamp). The offered stamp alone pins the content (a packwiz `pack.toml` hash or a Modrinth version id), and `changes`/`kept` are recomputed on every call from the disk and the current optional choices, so only the expensive `resolve` is memoized. A finished sync drops the instance's entry.

```rust
#[tokio::test]
async fn a_second_check_reuses_the_resolved_update() {
    let env = Env::new().await;
    let instance = synced(&env, v1(&env.server)).await;
    publish_v1_1(&env).await;
    let first = update_for(&env, &instance).await;
    let before = hits(&env.server, "/pack/pack.toml").await;
    let second = update_for(&env, &instance).await;
    assert_eq!(first, second);
    assert_eq!(
        hits(&env.server, "/pack/pack.toml").await - before,
        1,
        "only the cheap check fetched pack.toml again"
    );

    // Once the update is installed, the next check starts fresh.
    let lock = InstanceLock::try_acquire(&env.store.perland_dir(&instance.id)).unwrap();
    env.syncer
        .ensure_locked(&lock, &env.store, &instance.id, Some(second), &env.sink(), &Cancel::default())
        .await
        .unwrap();
    drop(lock);
    let fresh = env.store.load(&instance.id).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(env.syncer.find_update(&env.store, &fresh).await.unwrap(), None);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the memo**

In `crates/launcher-core/src/sync/mod.rs`:

1. Add `collections::HashMap` to the `std` imports (next to `BTreeMap`).
2. Add the field to `Syncer`:

```rust
pub struct Syncer {
    pub game: Arc<dyn GameInstall>,
    pub sources: Sources,
    /// The launcher's data folder; free space is measured here.
    pub root: PathBuf,
    /// Per instance: the last update `find_update` resolved (offered stamp, source, pack), so
    /// the banner and Play don't resolve the same version's metafiles twice.
    resolved: std::sync::Mutex<HashMap<String, (String, SourceRef, DesiredState)>>,
}
```

3. In `Syncer::new`, initialize `resolved: std::sync::Mutex::new(HashMap::new()),`.
4. In `find_update`, replace the part from `let source = match self.sources.check(...)` through `.map_err(update_check_failed)?;` with:

```rust
        let (source, stamp) = match self.sources.check(&instance.source, &installed).await {
            UpdateCheck::UpToDate => return Ok(None),
            UpdateCheck::Unavailable(e) => return Err(e),
            UpdateCheck::Available { source, stamp, .. } => (source, stamp),
        };
        let remembered = self
            .resolved
            .lock()
            .unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner())
            .get(&instance.id)
            .filter(|(offered, known, _)| *offered == stamp && *known == source)
            .map(|(_, _, desired)| desired.clone());
        let desired = match remembered {
            Some(desired) => desired,
            None => {
                let desired = self
                    .sources
                    .resolve(&source)
                    .await
                    .map_err(update_check_failed)?;
                self.resolved.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner()).insert(
                    instance.id.clone(),
                    (stamp, source.clone(), desired.clone()),
                );
                desired
            }
        };
```

5. At the end of a successful `sync_locked` (just before it returns the `SyncOutcome::Done(...)` for a sync that wrote files, and before the fast-path `Done` too), drop the entry:

```rust
        self.forget_update(id);
```

with the helper:

```rust
    /// A sync changed what's installed: the next update check resolves afresh.
    fn forget_update(&self, id: &str) {
        self.resolved
            .lock()
            .unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner())
            .remove(id);
    }
```

(`sync_locked` has several `return Ok(SyncOutcome::Done(...))` points; call `forget_update(id)` before each one that follows a completed sync. Read the function first: `crates/launcher-core/src/sync/mod.rs:478` onward.)

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the seam**

Create `crates/launcher-app/src/browser.rs`:

```rust
//! The seam between the app and Browse. The real one is `launcher_core::browse::Browse`; tests
//! use `FakeBrowser`.
use std::{path::PathBuf, sync::Arc};

use launcher_core::{
    browse::{
        compat::{loader_tag, ContentKind, Target},
        identify::Installed,
        images::{Image, ImageKind},
        plan::{InstallPlan, PlanItem, PlanRequest},
        project::{ProjectDetail, VersionInfo},
        search::{SearchPage, SearchQuery},
        Browse,
    },
    events::{EventSink, TaskId},
    meta::{catalog, loaders::LoaderKind, MetaClient},
    sync::lockfile::Managed,
    ErrorCode, LauncherError, Result,
};

use crate::runner::BoxFuture;

pub trait Browser: Send + Sync + 'static {
    fn search(&self, query: SearchQuery) -> BoxFuture<Result<SearchPage>>;
    fn categories(&self, kind: ContentKind) -> BoxFuture<Result<Vec<String>>>;
    fn project(&self, id: String) -> BoxFuture<Result<ProjectDetail>>;
    fn versions(&self, id: String, target: Option<Target>) -> BoxFuture<Result<Vec<VersionInfo>>>;
    fn image(&self, url: String, kind: ImageKind) -> BoxFuture<Result<Image>>;
    fn identify(&self, game_dir: PathBuf, managed: Option<Managed>) -> BoxFuture<Result<Installed>>;
    fn plan(
        &self,
        request: PlanRequest,
        target: Option<Target>,
        installed: Installed,
    ) -> BoxFuture<Result<InstallPlan>>;
    fn install(
        &self,
        game_dir: PathBuf,
        items: Vec<PlanItem>,
        managed: Option<Managed>,
        task: TaskId,
        sink: EventSink,
    ) -> BoxFuture<Result<Vec<String>>>;
    /// The loader version a new instance gets: the newest stable one, else the newest.
    fn loader_version(&self, kind: LoaderKind, minecraft: String) -> BoxFuture<Result<String>>;
}

pub struct CoreBrowser {
    browse: Arc<Browse>,
    meta: MetaClient,
}

impl CoreBrowser {
    pub fn new(browse: Browse, meta: MetaClient) -> Self {
        Self {
            browse: Arc::new(browse),
            meta,
        }
    }
}

impl Browser for CoreBrowser {
    fn search(&self, query: SearchQuery) -> BoxFuture<Result<SearchPage>> {
        let b = self.browse.clone();
        Box::pin(async move { b.search(&query).await })
    }
    fn categories(&self, kind: ContentKind) -> BoxFuture<Result<Vec<String>>> {
        let b = self.browse.clone();
        Box::pin(async move { b.categories(kind).await })
    }
    fn project(&self, id: String) -> BoxFuture<Result<ProjectDetail>> {
        let b = self.browse.clone();
        Box::pin(async move { b.project(&id).await })
    }
    fn versions(&self, id: String, target: Option<Target>) -> BoxFuture<Result<Vec<VersionInfo>>> {
        let b = self.browse.clone();
        Box::pin(async move { b.versions(&id, target.as_ref()).await })
    }
    fn image(&self, url: String, kind: ImageKind) -> BoxFuture<Result<Image>> {
        let b = self.browse.clone();
        Box::pin(async move { b.image(&url, kind).await })
    }
    fn identify(&self, game_dir: PathBuf, managed: Option<Managed>) -> BoxFuture<Result<Installed>> {
        let b = self.browse.clone();
        Box::pin(async move { b.identify(&game_dir, managed.as_ref()).await })
    }
    fn plan(
        &self,
        request: PlanRequest,
        target: Option<Target>,
        installed: Installed,
    ) -> BoxFuture<Result<InstallPlan>> {
        let b = self.browse.clone();
        Box::pin(async move { b.plan(&request, target.as_ref(), &installed).await })
    }
    fn install(
        &self,
        game_dir: PathBuf,
        items: Vec<PlanItem>,
        managed: Option<Managed>,
        task: TaskId,
        sink: EventSink,
    ) -> BoxFuture<Result<Vec<String>>> {
        let b = self.browse.clone();
        Box::pin(async move { b.install(&game_dir, &items, managed.as_ref(), &task, &sink).await })
    }
    fn loader_version(&self, kind: LoaderKind, minecraft: String) -> BoxFuture<Result<String>> {
        let meta = self.meta.clone();
        Box::pin(async move {
            let list = catalog::loader_versions(&meta, kind, &minecraft).await?;
            list.iter()
                .find(|v| v.stable)
                .or(list.first())
                .map(|v| v.version.clone())
                .ok_or_else(|| {
                    LauncherError::loader(
                        ErrorCode::LoaderVersionNotFound,
                        format!("No {} version for Minecraft {minecraft}", loader_tag(kind)),
                    )
                    .with_param("loader", loader_tag(kind))
                    .with_param("loader_version", "latest")
                    .with_param("version", &minecraft)
                })
        })
    }
}
```

Append to `crates/launcher-app/src/dto.rs` (add the imports to the `use` block at the top):

```rust
use launcher_core::{
    browse::{
        compat::ContentKind,
        plan::{ItemReason, PlanItem, PlanSkip},
        search::SortIndex,
    },
    instances::Instance,
    meta::loaders::LoaderKind,
};

/// Browse's search box and filters. With `instance`, its game decides `minecraft` and `loader`.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct SearchRequest {
    pub query: String,
    pub kind: ContentKind,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub instance: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub minecraft: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub loader: Option<LoaderKind>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub categories: Vec<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub sort: SortIndex,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub offset: u32,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(tag = "kind", rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum PlanTargetRequest {
    Instance { id: String },
    New,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct BrowsePlanRequest {
    pub project: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub version: Option<String>,
    pub target: PlanTargetRequest,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
#[serde(tag = "kind", rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum PlanTargetView {
    Instance {
        id: String,
        name: String,
        minecraft: String,
        loader: Option<LoaderKind>,
    },
    New {
        name: String,
        minecraft: String,
        loader: Option<LoaderKind>,
    },
}

/// One thing an install adds, as the confirm dialog shows it. URLs and hashes stay in the app.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct PlanItemView {
    pub project_id: String,
    pub title: String,
    pub kind: ContentKind,
    pub version_number: String,
    pub file_name: String,
    pub size: u64,
    pub reason: ItemReason,
}

impl From<&PlanItem> for PlanItemView {
    fn from(item: &PlanItem) -> Self {
        Self {
            project_id: item.project_id.clone(),
            title: item.title.clone(),
            kind: item.kind,
            version_number: item.version_number.clone(),
            file_name: item.file.file_name.clone(),
            size: item.file.size,
            reason: item.reason.clone(),
        }
    }
}

/// A stored install plan; the webview installs it by `plan_id` only.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct InstallPlanView {
    pub plan_id: String,
    pub target: PlanTargetView,
    pub items: Vec<PlanItemView>,
    pub skipped: Vec<PlanSkip>,
    pub total_size: u64,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct InstallResult {
    /// The instance the files went into (a new one for a "New instance" plan).
    pub instance: Instance,
    pub added: Vec<String>,
}
```

```rust
use launcher_core::browse::{
    compat::{ContentKind, Target},
    identify::Installed,
    images::{Image, ImageKind},
    plan::{InstallPlan, ItemReason, NewInstancePlan, PlanItem, PlanRequest, PlanSkip, PlannedFile, SkipReason},
    project::{ProjectDetail, VersionInfo},
    search::{SearchPage, SearchQuery},
};
use launcher_core::meta::loaders::LoaderKind;

use crate::browser::Browser;

/// Sodium (requested) and Fabric API (its dependency) for Fabric 1.20.1, with Mod Menu skipped
/// as part of the pack. `new_instance` makes it a plan for a new Fabric 1.21.1 instance.
pub(crate) fn sample_plan(new_instance: bool) -> InstallPlan {
    let file = |name: &str| PlannedFile {
        url: format!("https://cdn.modrinth.com/data/x/{name}"),
        file_name: name.into(),
        size: 100,
        hashes: Hashes::sha1("a".repeat(40)),
    };
    let item = |id: &str, title: &str, name: &str, reason: ItemReason| PlanItem {
        project_id: id.into(),
        title: title.into(),
        kind: ContentKind::Mod,
        version_id: format!("{id}-v"),
        version_number: "1.0".into(),
        reason,
        file: file(name),
    };
    let minecraft = if new_instance { "1.21.1" } else { "1.20.1" };
    InstallPlan {
        target: Target {
            minecraft: minecraft.into(),
            loader: Some(LoaderKind::Fabric),
        },
        new_instance: new_instance.then(|| NewInstancePlan {
            name: "Sodium".into(),
            minecraft: minecraft.into(),
            loader: Some(LoaderKind::Fabric),
        }),
        items: vec![
            item("SOD", "Sodium", "sodium.jar", ItemReason::Requested),
            item("API", "Fabric API", "fabric-api.jar", ItemReason::Dependency { of: "Sodium".into() }),
        ],
        skipped: vec![PlanSkip {
            project_id: "MENU".into(),
            title: "Mod Menu".into(),
            reason: SkipReason::InPack,
        }],
    }
}

/// Browse answers the test scripts. `install` records what it was asked and can be held open.
pub(crate) struct FakeBrowser {
    pub searches: Mutex<Vec<SearchQuery>>,
    pub installed: Mutex<Installed>,
    /// Each `identify` call: (game folder, whether a lockfile was passed).
    pub identified: Mutex<Vec<(PathBuf, bool)>>,
    /// What `plan` returns (none: `sample_plan` for the target it was given).
    pub plan_result: Mutex<Option<Result<InstallPlan>>>,
    pub plans: Mutex<Vec<(PlanRequest, Option<Target>)>>,
    /// Each `install` call: (game folder, file names).
    pub installs: Mutex<Vec<(PathBuf, Vec<String>)>>,
    pub install_error: Mutex<Option<LauncherError>>,
    /// While set, `install` waits for `open_install_gate`.
    pub hold_install: AtomicBool,
    install_gate: Arc<Semaphore>,
    pub image: Mutex<Option<Image>>,
}

impl FakeBrowser {
    pub fn new() -> Arc<Self> {
        Arc::new(Self {
            searches: Mutex::new(Vec::new()),
            installed: Mutex::new(Installed::default()),
            identified: Mutex::new(Vec::new()),
            plan_result: Mutex::new(None),
            plans: Mutex::new(Vec::new()),
            installs: Mutex::new(Vec::new()),
            install_error: Mutex::new(None),
            hold_install: AtomicBool::new(false),
            install_gate: Arc::new(Semaphore::new(0)),
            image: Mutex::new(None),
        })
    }

    pub fn open_install_gate(&self) {
        self.install_gate.add_permits(1);
    }
}

fn empty_page() -> SearchPage {
    SearchPage {
        hits: vec![],
        total: 0,
        offset: 0,
    }
}

impl Browser for FakeBrowser {
    fn search(&self, query: SearchQuery) -> BoxFuture<Result<SearchPage>> {
        self.searches.lock().unwrap().push(query);
        Box::pin(async { Ok(empty_page()) })
    }
    fn categories(&self, _kind: ContentKind) -> BoxFuture<Result<Vec<String>>> {
        Box::pin(async { Ok(vec!["adventure".to_string()]) })
    }
    fn project(&self, _id: String) -> BoxFuture<Result<ProjectDetail>> {
        Box::pin(async { Err(launcher_core::browse::api::not_found()) })
    }
    fn versions(&self, _id: String, _target: Option<Target>) -> BoxFuture<Result<Vec<VersionInfo>>> {
        Box::pin(async { Ok(vec![]) })
    }
    fn image(&self, _url: String, _kind: ImageKind) -> BoxFuture<Result<Image>> {
        let image = self.image.lock().unwrap().clone();
        Box::pin(async move {
            image.ok_or_else(|| LauncherError::network(ErrorCode::ImageRefused, "That image can't be shown"))
        })
    }
    fn identify(&self, game_dir: PathBuf, managed: Option<Managed>) -> BoxFuture<Result<Installed>> {
        self.identified.lock().unwrap().push((game_dir, managed.is_some()));
        let installed = self.installed.lock().unwrap().clone();
        Box::pin(async move { Ok(installed) })
    }
    fn plan(
        &self,
        request: PlanRequest,
        target: Option<Target>,
        _installed: Installed,
    ) -> BoxFuture<Result<InstallPlan>> {
        let new = target.is_none();
        self.plans.lock().unwrap().push((request, target));
        let scripted = self.plan_result.lock().unwrap().clone();
        Box::pin(async move { scripted.unwrap_or_else(|| Ok(sample_plan(new))) })
    }
    fn install(
        &self,
        game_dir: PathBuf,
        items: Vec<PlanItem>,
        _managed: Option<Managed>,
        task: TaskId,
        sink: EventSink,
    ) -> BoxFuture<Result<Vec<String>>> {
        let names: Vec<String> = items.iter().map(|i| i.file.file_name.clone()).collect();
        self.installs.lock().unwrap().push((game_dir, names.clone()));
        let error = self.install_error.lock().unwrap().take();
        let hold = self.hold_install.load(Ordering::SeqCst);
        let gate = self.install_gate.clone();
        Box::pin(async move {
            sink(Event::Progress {
                task,
                done: 0,
                total: names.len() as u64,
                bytes: 0,
            });
            if hold {
                gate.acquire().await.expect("never closed").forget();
            }
            match error {
                Some(e) => Err(e),
                None => Ok(names),
            }
        })
    }
    fn loader_version(&self, _kind: LoaderKind, _minecraft: String) -> BoxFuture<Result<String>> {
        Box::pin(async { Ok("0.16.14".to_string()) })
    }
}
```

```rust
        self.browser.clone()
    }
```

```rust
//! Browse commands (spec §4): search, project pages, images, what an instance has, and (in
//! Task 14) planned installs. All Modrinth work happens behind `Runner::browser`.
use base64::{engine::general_purpose::STANDARD, Engine};
use launcher_core::{
    browse::{
        compat::{ContentKind, Target},
        images::ImageKind,
        project::{ProjectDetail, VersionInfo},
        search::{SearchPage, SearchQuery},
    },
    sync::lockfile::Managed,
    ErrorCode, LauncherError, Result,
};

use crate::{app::AppCore, dto::SearchRequest, events::Host, runner::Runner};

/// The link a project page may open in the browser: https only, with a host and no user info.
pub fn link_to_open(raw: &str) -> Result<String> {
    let refused = || LauncherError::pack(ErrorCode::LinkRefused, "That link can't be opened");
    let url = url::Url::parse(raw.trim()).map_err(|_| refused())?;
    let plain = url.scheme() == "https"
        && url.host_str().is_some_and(|h| !h.is_empty())
        && url.username().is_empty()
        && url.password().is_none();
    if plain {
        Ok(url.to_string())
    } else {
        Err(refused())
    }
}

impl<R: Runner, H: Host> AppCore<R, H> {
    pub async fn browse_search(&self, request: SearchRequest) -> Result<SearchPage> {
        let (minecraft, loader) = match &request.instance {
            Some(id) => {
                let instance = self.instances.load(id).await?;
                (
                    Some(instance.game.minecraft),
                    instance.game.loader.map(|l| l.kind),
                )
            }
            None => (request.minecraft, request.loader),
        };
        self.runner
            .browser()
            .search(SearchQuery {
                query: request.query,
                kind: request.kind,
                minecraft,
                loader,
                categories: request.categories,
                sort: request.sort,
                offset: request.offset,
            })
            .await
    }

    pub async fn browse_categories(&self, kind: ContentKind) -> Result<Vec<String>> {
        self.runner.browser().categories(kind).await
    }

    pub async fn browse_project(&self, id: &str) -> Result<ProjectDetail> {
        self.runner.browser().project(id.to_string()).await
    }

    /// The project's versions; only those that fit `instance` when one is given.
    pub async fn browse_versions(
        &self,
        project: &str,
        instance: Option<&str>,
    ) -> Result<Vec<VersionInfo>> {
        let target = match instance {
            Some(id) => Some(Target::of(&self.instances.load(id).await?.game)),
            None => None,
        };
        self.runner
            .browser()
            .versions(project.to_string(), target)
            .await
    }

    /// An icon or gallery image as a `data:` URL (the CSP allows no remote images).
    pub async fn browse_image(&self, url: &str, kind: ImageKind) -> Result<String> {
        let image = self.runner.browser().image(url.to_string(), kind).await?;
        Ok(format!("data:{};base64,{}", image.mime, STANDARD.encode(&image.bytes)))
    }

    /// The Modrinth projects already in an instance, for "Installed" lamps.
    pub async fn browse_installed(&self, id: &str) -> Result<Vec<String>> {
        let instance = self.instances.load(id).await?;
        let managed = Managed::load(&self.instances.perland_dir(&instance.id)).await;
        let installed = self
            .runner
            .browser()
            .identify(self.instances.game_dir(&instance.id), managed)
            .await?;
        Ok(installed.project_ids())
    }
}
```

```rust
    #[tokio::test]
    async fn stopping_an_update_only_run_says_it_was_one() {
        let (core, runner, host, _t) = ready(true).await;
        let id = pack(&core, UpdatePolicy::Ask).await;
        offer(&runner, &id);
        runner.hold_sync.store(true, std::sync::atomic::Ordering::SeqCst);
        // The banner's Update: checking, then queued/syncing. Stop while it is still checking
        // or queued must say it was an update-only run.
        core.pack_update(&id).await.unwrap();
        core.stop(&id).unwrap();
        host.wait_for(&id, Idle).await;
        let exit = host.last_exit(&id).unwrap();
        assert!(exit.cancelled);
        assert!(exit.update_only, "an update-only run stays update-only when stopped");

        core.play(&id).await.unwrap();
        host.wait_for(&id, Asking).await;
        core.stop(&id).unwrap();
        host.wait_for_nth(&id, Idle, 2).await;
        assert!(!host.last_exit(&id).unwrap().update_only, "Play is not update-only");
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write planning and installing**

Add to `crates/launcher-app/src/browse.rs` (extend the `use` block with what's named here):

```rust
use std::{
    sync::{atomic::Ordering, Arc, Mutex},
    time::Duration,
};

use launcher_core::{
    browse::{
        identify::Installed,
        plan::{InstallPlan, PlanRequest},
    },
    events::{Event, EventSink, TaskId},
    instances::{GameSpec, Instance, LoaderSpec},
    locks::InstanceLock,
};
use tokio::time::Instant;

use crate::{
    dto::{
        BrowsePlanRequest, InstallPlanView, InstallResult, NewInstance, PlanItemView,
        PlanTargetRequest, PlanTargetView,
    },
    events::{AppEvent, RunStateKind},
};

/// How long a plan stays installable.
pub const PLAN_TTL: Duration = Duration::from_secs(10 * 60);

pub(crate) struct StoredPlan {
    plan: InstallPlan,
    /// The target instance, and its game when planned (a changed game makes the plan stale).
    instance: Option<(String, GameSpec)>,
    created: Instant,
}

fn plan_expired() -> LauncherError {
    LauncherError::pack(
        ErrorCode::ContentPlanExpired,
        "That took too long. Try installing again",
    )
}

fn content_busy(name: &str) -> LauncherError {
    LauncherError::pack(
        ErrorCode::ContentBusy,
        format!("Wait for {name} to finish updating"),
    )
    .with_param("name", name)
}

pub(crate) fn content_installing() -> LauncherError {
    LauncherError::pack(ErrorCode::ContentInstalling, "Wait for the download to finish")
}

/// Marks an instance as being installed into; holds the instance lock when no run does.
pub(crate) struct ContentGuard<'a> {
    installing: &'a Mutex<HashSet<String>>,
    id: String,
    _lock: Option<InstanceLock>,
}

impl Drop for ContentGuard<'_> {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        self.installing
            .lock()
            .unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner())
            .remove(&self.id);
    }
}

impl<R: Runner, H: Host> AppCore<R, H> {
    /// Spec §4.2: refused while the instance updates; fine while its game prepares or runs
    /// (the run holds the lock, and installs only add files); otherwise take the lock.
    fn content_guard(&self, instance: &Instance) -> Result<ContentGuard<'_>> {
        let runs = self.runs.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner());
        let mut installing = self.installing.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner());
        if installing.contains(&instance.id) {
            return Err(content_installing());
        }
        let lock = match runs.get(&instance.id).map(|run| run.phase) {
            Some(
                RunStateKind::Checking
                | RunStateKind::Asking
                | RunStateKind::Queued
                | RunStateKind::Syncing,
            ) => return Err(content_busy(&instance.name)),
            Some(_) => None,
            None => Some(
                InstanceLock::try_acquire(&self.instances.perland_dir(&instance.id)).map_err(
                    |e| {
                        if e.code == ErrorCode::InstanceLocked {
                            content_busy(&instance.name)
                        } else {
                            e
                        }
                    },
                )?,
            ),
        };
        installing.insert(instance.id.clone());
        Ok(ContentGuard {
            installing: &self.installing,
            id: instance.id.clone(),
            _lock: lock,
        })
    }

    /// Install progress, tagged with the instance (the UI shows it on the install button).
    fn content_sink(&self, id: &str) -> EventSink {
        let (host, id) = (self.host.clone(), id.to_string());
        Arc::new(move |event| {
            if let Event::Progress {
                task,
                done,
                total,
                bytes,
            } = event
            {
                host.emit(AppEvent::Progress {
                    instance: Some(id.clone()),
                    task,
                    done,
                    total,
                    bytes,
                });
            }
        })
    }

    pub async fn browse_plan(&self, request: BrowsePlanRequest) -> Result<InstallPlanView> {
        let browser = self.runner.browser();
        let wanted = PlanRequest {
            project: request.project,
            version: request.version,
        };
        let (instance, plan) = match request.target {
            PlanTargetRequest::Instance { id } => {
                let instance = self.instances.load(&id).await?;
                let managed = Managed::load(&self.instances.perland_dir(&instance.id)).await;
                let installed = browser
                    .identify(self.instances.game_dir(&instance.id), managed)
                    .await?;
                let plan = browser
                    .plan(wanted, Some(Target::of(&instance.game)), installed)
                    .await?;
                (Some(instance), plan)
            }
            PlanTargetRequest::New => (
                None,
                browser.plan(wanted, None, Installed::default()).await?,
            ),
        };
        let target = match (&instance, &plan.new_instance) {
            (Some(i), _) => PlanTargetView::Instance {
                id: i.id.clone(),
                name: i.name.clone(),
                minecraft: i.game.minecraft.clone(),
                loader: i.game.loader.as_ref().map(|l| l.kind),
            },
            (None, Some(new)) => PlanTargetView::New {
                name: new.name.clone(),
                minecraft: new.minecraft.clone(),
                loader: new.loader,
            },
            (None, None) => return Err(plan_expired()),
        };
        let plan_id = format!("plan-{}", self.plan_seq.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed) + 1);
        let view = InstallPlanView {
            plan_id: plan_id.clone(),
            target,
            items: plan.items.iter().map(PlanItemView::from).collect(),
            skipped: plan.skipped.clone(),
            total_size: plan.items.iter().map(|i| i.file.size).sum(),
        };
        let mut plans = self.plans.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner());
        plans.retain(|_, p| p.created.elapsed() < PLAN_TTL);
        plans.insert(
            plan_id,
            StoredPlan {
                plan,
                instance: instance.map(|i| (i.id, i.game)),
                created: Instant::now(),
            },
        );
        Ok(view)
    }

    /// Install a stored plan, once. A "New instance" plan creates the instance first; if the
    /// install then fails, the instance stays so the player can retry from it.
    pub async fn browse_install(&self, plan_id: &str) -> Result<InstallResult> {
        let stored = self
            .plans
            .lock()
            .unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner())
            .remove(plan_id)
            .filter(|p| p.created.elapsed() < PLAN_TTL)
            .ok_or_else(plan_expired)?;
        let browser = self.runner.browser();
        let instance = match &stored.instance {
            Some((id, game)) => {
                let instance = self.instances.load(id).await?;
                if instance.game != *game {
                    return Err(plan_expired());
                }
                instance
            }
            None => {
                let new = stored.plan.new_instance.clone().ok_or_else(plan_expired)?;
                let loader = match new.loader {
                    Some(kind) => Some(LoaderSpec {
                        kind,
                        version: browser.loader_version(kind, new.minecraft.clone()).await?,
                    }),
                    None => None,
                };
                self.instance_create(NewInstance {
                    name: new.name,
                    minecraft: new.minecraft,
                    loader,
                })
                .await?
            }
        };
        let _guard = self.content_guard(&instance)?;
        let managed = Managed::load(&self.instances.perland_dir(&instance.id)).await;
        let title = stored
            .plan
            .items
            .first()
            .map(|i| i.title.clone())
            .unwrap_or_default();
        let added = browser
            .install(
                self.instances.game_dir(&instance.id),
                stored.plan.items,
                managed,
                TaskId::new("install_content").with("name", &title),
                self.content_sink(&instance.id),
            )
            .await?;
        Ok(InstallResult { instance, added })
    }
}
```

(Add `std::collections::HashSet` to the imports.)

### Task 15: Tauri wiring

**Files:**
- Modify: `src-tauri/src/commands.rs`
- Modify: `src-tauri/src/main.rs` (`generate_handler!`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes every `AppCore` browse command (Tasks 12, 14) and `launcher_app::browse::link_to_open`.
- Produces Tauri commands `browse_search`, `browse_categories`, `browse_project`, `browse_versions`, `browse_image`, `browse_installed`, `browse_plan`, `browse_install`, `open_link`. JS argument names are camelCase (`planId`), as Tauri maps them to the snake_case Rust parameters.

`src-tauri` has no tests (`test = false`); the commands are thin forwards, verified by `cargo build` and the owner smoke test.

- [ ] **Step 1: Add the commands**

Append to `src-tauri/src/commands.rs` (extend the imports):

```rust
use launcher_app::dto::{BrowsePlanRequest, InstallPlanView, InstallResult, SearchRequest};
use launcher_core::browse::{
    compat::ContentKind,
    images::ImageKind,
    project::{ProjectDetail, VersionInfo},
    search::SearchPage,
};

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn browse_search(core: CoreState<'_>, request: SearchRequest) -> Res<SearchPage> {
    core.browse_search(request).await
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn browse_categories(core: CoreState<'_>, kind: ContentKind) -> Res<Vec<String>> {
    core.browse_categories(kind).await
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn browse_project(core: CoreState<'_>, id: String) -> Res<ProjectDetail> {
    core.browse_project(&id).await
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn browse_versions(
    core: CoreState<'_>,
    project: String,
    instance: Option<String>,
) -> Res<Vec<VersionInfo>> {
    core.browse_versions(&project, instance.as_deref()).await
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn browse_image(core: CoreState<'_>, url: String, kind: ImageKind) -> Res<String> {
    core.browse_image(&url, kind).await
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn browse_installed(core: CoreState<'_>, id: String) -> Res<Vec<String>> {
    core.browse_installed(&id).await
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn browse_plan(core: CoreState<'_>, request: BrowsePlanRequest) -> Res<InstallPlanView> {
    core.browse_plan(request).await
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn browse_install(core: CoreState<'_>, plan_id: String) -> Res<InstallResult> {
    core.browse_install(&plan_id).await
}

/// Project and Markdown links: https only (checked in `launcher-app`), opened from Rust, so the
/// webview's opener scope stays as narrow as it was.
#[tauri::command]
pub fn open_link(app: AppHandle, url: String) -> Res<()> {
    let url = launcher_app::browse::link_to_open(&url)?;
    app.opener()
        .open_url(url, None::<&str>)
        .map_err(|e| LauncherError::pack(ErrorCode::LinkRefused, "That link can't be opened").with_detail(e))
}
```

Register all nine in `generate_handler!` in `src-tauri/src/main.rs`, after `commands::deep_link_take,`.

- [ ] **Step 4: Add the schemas and commands**

Append to `ui/src/ipc/schema.ts` (before the `export type` block; add the matching `export type X = z.infer<typeof x>;` lines to it):

```ts
export const contentKind = z.enum(['modpack', 'mod', 'resource_pack', 'shader']);
export const sortIndex = z.enum(['relevance', 'downloads', 'follows', 'newest', 'updated']);
export const imageKind = z.enum(['icon', 'gallery']);
export const searchHit = z.strictObject({
  projectId: z.string(),
  slug: z.string(),
  title: z.string(),
  author: z.string(),
  summary: z.string(),
  iconUrl: z.string().nullable(),
  downloads: z.number().int(),
  follows: z.number().int(),
  categories: z.array(z.string()),
  kind: contentKind,
});
export const searchPage = z.strictObject({ hits: z.array(searchHit), total: z.number().int(), offset: z.number().int() });
export const sideSupport = z.enum(['required', 'optional', 'unsupported', 'unknown']);
export const galleryImage = z.strictObject({ url: z.string(), title: z.string().nullable(), featured: z.boolean() });
export const projectDetail = z.strictObject({
  projectId: z.string(),
  slug: z.string(),
  title: z.string(),
  author: z.string().nullable(),
  summary: z.string(),
  body: z.string(),
  iconUrl: z.string().nullable(),
  downloads: z.number().int(),
  follows: z.number().int(),
  categories: z.array(z.string()),
  kind: contentKind,
  clientSide: sideSupport,
  serverSide: sideSupport,
  license: z.string().nullable(),
  sourceUrl: z.string().nullable(),
  issuesUrl: z.string().nullable(),
  wikiUrl: z.string().nullable(),
  gallery: z.array(galleryImage),
});
export const versionInfo = z.strictObject({
  id: z.string(),
  name: z.string(),
  number: z.string(),
  versionType: z.string(),
  gameVersions: z.array(z.string()),
  loaders: z.array(z.string()),
  date: z.string(),
  size: z.number().int().nullable(),
});
export const itemReason = z.discriminatedUnion('kind', [
  z.strictObject({ kind: z.literal('requested') }),
  z.strictObject({ kind: z.literal('dependency'), of: z.string() }),
  z.strictObject({ kind: z.literal('shader_mod') }),
]);
export const planSkip = z.strictObject({ projectId: z.string(), title: z.string(), reason: z.enum(['installed', 'in_pack']) });
export const planItemView = z.strictObject({
  projectId: z.string(),
  title: z.string(),
  kind: contentKind,
  versionNumber: z.string(),
  fileName: z.string(),
  size: z.number().int(),
  reason: itemReason,
});
export const planTargetView = z.discriminatedUnion('kind', [
  z.strictObject({ kind: z.literal('instance'), id: z.string(), name: z.string(), minecraft: z.string(), loader: loaderKind.nullable() }),
  z.strictObject({ kind: z.literal('new'), name: z.string(), minecraft: z.string(), loader: loaderKind.nullable() }),
]);
export const installPlanView = z.strictObject({
  planId: z.string(),
  target: planTargetView,
  items: z.array(planItemView),
  skipped: z.array(planSkip),
  totalSize: z.number().int(),
});
export const installResult = z.strictObject({ instance, added: z.array(z.string()) });
export const searchRequest = z.strictObject({
  query: z.string(),
  kind: contentKind,
  instance: z.string().nullable(),
  minecraft: z.string().nullable(),
  loader: loaderKind.nullable(),
  categories: z.array(z.string()),
  sort: sortIndex,
  offset: z.number().int(),
});
export const browsePlanRequest = z.strictObject({
  project: z.string(),
  version: z.string().nullable(),
  target: z.discriminatedUnion('kind', [
    z.strictObject({ kind: z.literal('instance'), id: z.string() }),
    z.strictObject({ kind: z.literal('new') }),
  ]),
});
```

```ts
  browseSearch: (request: SearchRequest) => invoke<SearchPage>('browse_search', { request }),

  browseInstalled: (id: string) => invoke<string[]>('browse_installed', { id }),
  browsePlan: (request: BrowsePlanRequest) => invoke<InstallPlanView>('browse_plan', { request }),
  browseInstall: (planId: string) => invoke<InstallResult>('browse_install', { planId }),
  openLink: (url: string) => invoke<null>('open_link', { url }),
```

```tsx
t('play.files', { count: progress.total, done: formatCount(progress.done, lang), total: formatCount(progress.total, lang) })
```

(In `PlayButton` the key is chosen by `progress.total >= 1000 ? 'play.filesShort' : 'play.files'`; passing `count` to `filesShort` is harmless, it has no plural forms. `ActivityDrawer` uses `i18n.language` for `lang`.)

### Task 17: UI compatibility helpers and the mock Browse backend

**Files:**
- Create: `ui/src/features/browse/compat.ts`, `ui/src/features/browse/compat.test.ts`
- Modify: `ui/src/ipc/mock.ts`
- Test: `ui/src/ipc/mock.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes the schema types (Task 16).
- Produces in `ui/src/features/browse/compat.ts`:
  - `loaderTags(loader: LoaderKind, minecraft: string): string[]`
  - `fitsVersion(v: VersionInfo, kind: ContentKind, instance: Instance): boolean`
  - `type Fit = { ok: true } | { ok: false; reason: 'needsLoader' | 'noVersion' }` and `fitFor(versions: VersionInfo[], kind: ContentKind, instance: Instance): Fit`
  - `pickDefault(versions: VersionInfo[]): VersionInfo | undefined`
- Produces mock commands `browse_search`, `browse_categories`, `browse_project`, `browse_versions`, `browse_image`, `browse_installed`, `browse_plan`, `browse_install`, `open_link`, with magic queries `offline` (→ `host_unreachable`) and `busy` (→ `browse_rate_limited`, `seconds: "3"`). The mock catalog: Sodium (`AANobbMI`, mod, Fabric/Quilt 1.20.1, needs Fabric API), Fabric API (`P7dR8mSH`, mod, already in `asalith-fields`), Iris (`YL57xq9U`, mod, needs Sodium), Complementary (`HVnmMxH1`, shader), Faithful (`FAITHFUL`, resource pack), Fabulously Optimized (`1KVo5zza`, modpack), and 24 filler mods `Mod 1`…`Mod 24` (`FILL0001`…) so paging has a second page.

These helpers mirror `launcher-core::browse::compat::fits` for the UI's picker; the core re-checks every plan, so a mismatch can only disable an option, never install something unfit.
