```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { Instance, VersionInfo } from '../../ipc/schema';
import { fitFor, fitsVersion, loaderTags, pickDefault } from './compat';

const instance = (minecraft: string, loader: Instance['game']['loader']): Instance => ({
  id: 'i',
  name: 'I',
  source: { kind: 'none' },
  game: { minecraft, loader },
  updatePolicy: 'ask',
  optionalChoices: {},
  java: { path: null, memoryMb: 4096 },
});
const version = (id: string, type: string, date: string, mc: string[], loaders: string[]): VersionInfo => ({
  id,
  name: id,
  number: id,
  versionType: type,
  gameVersions: mc,
  loaders,
  date,
  size: 1,
});
const fabric = instance('1.20.1', { kind: 'fabric', version: '0.16.14' });
const quilt = instance('1.20.1', { kind: 'quilt', version: '0.24.0' });
const vanilla = instance('1.20.1', null);

describe('compat', () => {
  it('follows the core loader rules', () => {
    expect(loaderTags('quilt', '1.20.1')).toEqual(['quilt', 'fabric']);
    expect(loaderTags('neoforge', '1.20.1')).toEqual(['neoforge', 'forge']);
    expect(loaderTags('neoforge', '1.21.1')).toEqual(['neoforge']);
  });

  it('fits mods, shaders and resource packs', () => {
    const fabricMod = version('a', 'release', '2026-01-01', ['1.20.1'], ['fabric']);
    expect(fitsVersion(fabricMod, 'mod', quilt)).toBe(true);
    expect(fitsVersion(fabricMod, 'mod', vanilla)).toBe(false);
    expect(fitsVersion(version('s', 'release', 'd', ['1.20.1'], ['iris']), 'shader', fabric)).toBe(true);
    expect(fitsVersion(version('s', 'release', 'd', ['1.20.1'], ['iris']), 'shader', vanilla)).toBe(false);
    expect(fitsVersion(version('r', 'release', 'd', ['1.20.1'], ['minecraft']), 'resource_pack', vanilla)).toBe(true);
    expect(fitFor([fabricMod], 'mod', vanilla)).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: 'needsLoader' });
    expect(fitFor([fabricMod], 'mod', instance('1.21.1', { kind: 'fabric', version: '1' }))).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: 'noVersion' });
    expect(fitFor([fabricMod], 'mod', fabric)).toEqual({ ok: true });
  });

  it('defaults to the newest release, then beta, then alpha', () => {
    const list = [
      version('beta-new', 'beta', '2026-03-01', [], []),
      version('rel-old', 'release', '2026-01-01', [], []),
      version('rel-new', 'release', '2026-02-01', [], []),
    ];
    expect(pickDefault(list)?.id).toBe('rel-new');
    expect(pickDefault(list.slice(0, 1))?.id).toBe('beta-new');
    expect(pickDefault([])).toBeUndefined();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the helpers**

```ts
// The UI's copy of launcher-core's `browse::compat` rules, for the "Install to" picker. The core
// re-checks every plan, so a mismatch here only disables an option.
import type { ContentKind, Instance, LoaderKind, VersionInfo } from '../../ipc/schema';

export function loaderTags(loader: LoaderKind, minecraft: string): string[] {
  if (loader === 'quilt') return ['quilt', 'fabric'];
  if (loader === 'neoforge' && minecraft === '1.20.1') return ['neoforge', 'forge'];
  return [loader];
}

export function fitsVersion(v: VersionInfo, kind: ContentKind, instance: Instance): boolean {
  const { minecraft, loader } = instance.game;
  if (!v.gameVersions.includes(minecraft)) return false;
  switch (kind) {
    case 'mod':
      return loader !== null && loaderTags(loader.kind, minecraft).some((tag) => v.loaders.includes(tag));
    case 'shader':
      return loader !== null && v.loaders.includes('iris');
    case 'resource_pack':
      return true;
    case 'modpack':
      return false;
  }
}

export type Fit = { ok: true } | { ok: false; reason: 'needsLoader' | 'noVersion' };

export function fitFor(versions: VersionInfo[], kind: ContentKind, instance: Instance): Fit {
  if ((kind === 'mod' || kind === 'shader') && instance.game.loader === null) return { ok: false, reason: 'needsLoader' };
  return versions.some((v) => fitsVersion(v, kind, instance)) ? { ok: true } : { ok: false, reason: 'noVersion' };
}

const stability = (type: string) => (type === 'release' ? 0 : type === 'beta' ? 1 : 2);

/** Releases first, then betas, then alphas; the newest within each. */
export function pickDefault(versions: VersionInfo[]): VersionInfo | undefined {
  return [...versions].sort((a, b) => stability(a.versionType) - stability(b.versionType) || b.date.localeCompare(a.date))[0];
}
```

In `ui/src/ipc/mock.ts`:

2. Add the catalog above `seed`:

```ts
// A tiny Modrinth for the browser build and the tests. Icons are one inline SVG.
const MOCK_ICON =
  'data:image/svg+xml;base64,' +
  btoa('<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 8 8"><rect width="8" height="8" fill="#c9cc2a"/></svg>');

interface MockProject {
  detail: ProjectDetail;
  versions: VersionInfo[];
  /** Project ids every version requires. */
  deps: string[];
}

function mockProject(
  projectId: string,
  title: string,
  kind: ContentKind,
  loaders: string[],
  deps: string[] = [],
  extra: Partial<ProjectDetail> = {},
): MockProject {
  const base = clone(projectDetailFx) as ProjectDetail;
  const version = (clone(versionsFx) as VersionInfo[])[0]!;
  return {
    detail: { ...base, projectId, slug: title.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, '-'), title, kind, categories: [], gallery: [], ...extra },
    versions: [{ ...version, id: `${projectId}-v1`, name: `${title} 1.0`, number: '1.0', loaders, gameVersions: ['1.20.1', '1.21.1'] }],
    deps,
  };
}

const CATALOG: MockProject[] = [
  { ...mockProject('AANobbMI', 'Sodium', 'mod', ['fabric', 'quilt'], ['P7dR8mSH']), detail: clone(projectDetailFx) as ProjectDetail },
  mockProject('P7dR8mSH', 'Fabric API', 'mod', ['fabric'], [], { categories: ['library'] }),
  mockProject('YL57xq9U', 'Iris', 'mod', ['fabric', 'quilt', 'neoforge'], ['AANobbMI']),
  mockProject('HVnmMxH1', 'Complementary', 'shader', ['iris']),
  mockProject('FAITHFUL', 'Faithful', 'resource_pack', ['minecraft']),
  mockProject('1KVo5zza', 'Fabulously Optimized', 'modpack', ['fabric']),
  ...Array.from({ length: 24 }, (_, i) =>
    mockProject(`FILL${String(i + 1).padStart(4, '0')}`, `Mod ${i + 1}`, 'mod', ['fabric', 'forge']),
  ),
];
// Sodium's fixture versions only list 1.20.1; keep its id matching the catalog's.
CATALOG[0]!.versions = (clone(versionsFx) as VersionInfo[]).map((v) => ({ ...v, id: 'AANobbMI-v1' }));

const findProject = (ref: string) => CATALOG.find((p) => p.detail.projectId === ref || p.detail.slug === ref);
```

4. Add a helper for plans and the cases in `handle`:

```ts
function planFor(state: MockState, request: BrowsePlanRequest): InstallPlanView {
  const project = findProject(request.project);
  if (!project) fail('pack', 'content_not_found', "That project wasn't found");
  const { detail } = project;
  if (detail.kind === 'modpack') fail('pack', 'content_incompatible', 'Modpacks become instances', { name: detail.title });
  const targetId = request.target.kind === 'instance' ? request.target.id : null;
  const instance = targetId ? state.listing.instances.find((i) => i.id === targetId) : undefined;
  if (targetId && !instance) fail('disk', 'instance_not_found', 'No such instance');
  if (instance) {
    const fit = fitFor(project.versions, detail.kind, instance);
    if (!fit.ok)
      fail('pack', fit.reason === 'needsLoader' ? 'content_needs_loader' : 'content_incompatible', 'Not for this instance', {
        name: detail.title,
      });
  }
  const have = instance ? (state.browse.installed.get(instance.id) ?? new Set<string>()) : new Set<string>();
  if (have.has(detail.projectId)) fail('pack', 'content_already_installed', 'Already there', { name: detail.title });
  const item = (p: MockProject, reason: PlanItemView['reason']): PlanItemView => ({
    projectId: p.detail.projectId,
    title: p.detail.title,
    kind: p.detail.kind,
    versionNumber: p.versions[0]!.number,
    fileName: `${p.detail.slug}.${p.detail.kind === 'mod' ? 'jar' : 'zip'}`,
    size: p.versions[0]!.size ?? 0,
    reason,
  });
  const items = [item(project, { kind: 'requested' })];
  const skipped: PlanSkip[] = [];
  const queue = [...project.deps.map((id) => ({ id, of: detail.title }))];
  if (detail.kind === 'shader') queue.push({ id: 'YL57xq9U', of: '' });
  const seen = new Set([detail.projectId]);
  while (queue.length > 0) {
    const next = queue.shift()!;
    if (seen.has(next.id)) continue;
    seen.add(next.id);
    const dep = findProject(next.id)!;
    if (have.has(next.id)) {
      skipped.push({ projectId: next.id, title: dep.detail.title, reason: 'installed' });
      continue;
    }
    items.push(item(dep, next.of === '' ? { kind: 'shader_mod' } : { kind: 'dependency', of: next.of }));
    queue.push(...dep.deps.map((id) => ({ id, of: dep.detail.title })));
  }
  const version = request.version ? project.versions.find((v) => v.id === request.version) : pickDefault(project.versions);
  const minecraft = instance?.game.minecraft ?? version?.gameVersions.at(-1) ?? '1.21.1';
  const target: InstallPlanView['target'] = instance
    ? { kind: 'instance', id: instance.id, name: instance.name, minecraft, loader: instance.game.loader?.kind ?? null }
    : { kind: 'new', name: detail.title, minecraft, loader: detail.kind === 'resource_pack' ? null : 'fabric' };
  const planId = `plan-${state.browse.nextPlan++}`;
  const view: InstallPlanView = { planId, target, items, skipped, totalSize: items.reduce((sum, i) => sum + i.size, 0) };
  state.browse.plans.set(planId, { view, kind: detail.kind });
  return view;
}
```

```ts
    case 'browse_search': {
      const request = args.request as SearchRequest;
      if (request.query === 'offline') fail('network', 'host_unreachable', "Couldn't reach api.modrinth.com", { host: 'api.modrinth.com' });
      if (request.query === 'busy') fail('network', 'browse_rate_limited', 'Too many searches', { seconds: '3' });
      const scoped = request.instance ? state.listing.instances.find((i) => i.id === request.instance) : undefined;
      const matches = CATALOG.filter(
        (p) =>
          p.detail.kind === request.kind &&
          p.detail.title.toLowerCase().includes(request.query.trim().toLowerCase()) &&
          request.categories.every((c) => p.detail.categories.includes(c)) &&
          (!scoped || p.versions.some((v) => fitsVersion(v, p.detail.kind, scoped))),
      );
      const hits: SearchHit[] = matches.slice(request.offset, request.offset + 20).map((p) => ({
        projectId: p.detail.projectId,
        slug: p.detail.slug,
        title: p.detail.title,
        author: p.detail.author ?? '',
        summary: p.detail.summary,
        iconUrl: p.detail.iconUrl,
        downloads: p.detail.downloads,
        follows: p.detail.follows,
        categories: p.detail.categories,
        kind: p.detail.kind,
      }));
      return { hits, total: matches.length, offset: request.offset } satisfies SearchPage;
    }
    case 'browse_categories':
      return args.kind === 'mod' ? ['library', 'optimization', 'technology'] : ['adventure'];
    case 'browse_project': {
      const project = findProject(args.id as string);
      if (!project) fail('pack', 'content_not_found', "That project wasn't found");
      return project.detail;
    }
    case 'browse_versions':
      return findProject(args.project as string)?.versions ?? [];
    case 'browse_image':
      return MOCK_ICON;
    case 'browse_installed':
      return [...(state.browse.installed.get(args.id as string) ?? [])].sort();
    case 'browse_plan':
      return planFor(state, args.request as BrowsePlanRequest);
    case 'browse_install': {
      const stored = state.browse.plans.get(args.planId as string);
      if (!stored) fail('pack', 'content_plan_expired', 'That took too long');
      state.browse.plans.delete(args.planId as string);
      const { view } = stored;
      let instance: Instance;
      if (view.target.kind === 'instance') {
        instance = state.listing.instances.find((i) => i.id === (view.target as { id: string }).id)!;
      } else {
        instance = (await handle(state, 'instance_create', {
          request: {
            name: view.target.name,
            minecraft: view.target.minecraft,
            loader: view.target.loader ? { kind: view.target.loader, version: '0.16.14' } : null,
          },
        })) as Instance;
      }
      const have = state.browse.installed.get(instance.id) ?? new Set<string>();
      view.items.forEach((item) => have.add(item.projectId));
      state.browse.installed.set(instance.id, have);
      const task = { key: 'install_content', params: { name: view.items[0]!.title } };
      event({ type: 'progress', instance: instance.id, task, done: view.items.length, total: view.items.length, bytes: view.totalSize });
      return { instance, added: view.items.map((i) => i.fileName) } satisfies InstallResult;
    }
    case 'open_link':
      state.browse.opened.push(args.url as string);
      return null;
```

(`instanceOf` looks for `id`, so Browse never changes the rail's selected cabinet.)

`ui/src/features/browse/session.ts`:

```ts
// Browse remembers its last kind and each kind's filters for the session (in memory only).
import type { ContentKind, LoaderKind, SortIndex } from '../../ipc/schema';

export interface Filters {
  query: string;
  /** null: Downloads for an empty query, Best match otherwise. */
  sort: SortIndex | null;
  minecraft: string | null;
  loader: LoaderKind | null;
  categories: string[];
}

const EMPTY: Filters = { query: '', sort: null, minecraft: null, loader: null, categories: [] };
let lastKind: ContentKind = 'modpack';
const byKind = new Map<string, Filters>();

export const sessionKind = (): ContentKind => lastKind;

export function sessionFilters(kind: ContentKind, scoped: boolean): Filters {
  return byKind.get(`${kind}:${scoped}`) ?? EMPTY;
}

export function remember(kind: ContentKind, scoped: boolean, filters: Filters) {
  if (!scoped) lastKind = kind;
  byKind.set(`${kind}:${scoped}`, filters);
}

export function resetBrowseSession() {
  lastKind = 'modpack';
  byKind.clear();
}
```

Call `resetBrowseSession()` and `resetContentProgress()` wherever `ui/src/test/setup.ts` resets the other module stores between tests (it already resets the route and toasts; add these two next to them).

`ui/src/state/toasts.ts`:

```ts
export interface Notice {
  title: string;
  body?: string;
  action?: { label: string; run: () => void };
}

export interface Toast {
  id: number;
  error?: unknown;
  notice?: Notice;
}

export function pushNotice(notice: Notice) {
  toasts = [...toasts, { id: nextId++, notice }];
  notify();
}
```

(`pushError` keeps pushing `{ id, error }`.)

`ui/src/ui/Toasts.tsx`: `ToastItem` renders a notice when `toast.notice` is set: a lime `Check` icon instead of the coral diamond, `notice.title` in bold, `notice.body` dim, an optional secondary `Button` with `notice.action.label` that runs the action and dismisses the toast, and the same dismiss button and 8 s timer. Errors render as today.

```tsx
function ToastItem({ toast }: { toast: Toast }) {
  const { t, i18n } = useTranslation();
  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = setTimeout(() => dismissToast(toast.id), 8000);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [toast.id]);
  const notice = toast.notice;
  const text = notice ? { message: notice.title, fix: notice.body ?? null } : describeError(i18n, toast.error);
  return (
    <li className="panel flex items-start gap-3 p-4 shadow-[0_6px_0_var(--color-dirt-deep)]">
      {notice ? (
        <Check className="mt-0.5 h-5 w-5 flex-none text-lime" aria-hidden />
      ) : (
        <WarningDiamond className="mt-0.5 h-5 w-5 flex-none text-coral" aria-hidden />
      )}
      <div className="grid flex-1 gap-0.5">
        <p className="font-semibold">{text.message}</p>
        {text.fix && <p className="text-sm text-ink-dim">{text.fix}</p>}
        {notice?.action && (
          <div className="pt-2">
            <Button
              onClick={() => {
                notice.action?.run();
                dismissToast(toast.id);
              }}
            >
              {notice.action.label}
            </Button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
      <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" aria-label={t('common.dismiss')} icon={<Close />} onClick={() => dismissToast(toast.id)} />
    </li>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Install progress store and queries**

`ui/src/state/contentProgress.ts`:

```ts
// Progress of Browse installs, per instance. Kept apart from runStore so an install never
// shows on Play.
import { useSyncExternalStore } from 'react';
import type { AppEvent } from '../ipc/schema';
import type { ProgressView } from './runStore';

let progress = new Map<string, ProgressView>();
const listeners = new Set<() => void>();
const notify = () => listeners.forEach((listener) => listener());

export function applyContentProgress(event: Extract<AppEvent, { type: 'progress' }>) {
  if (!event.instance) return;
  const { task, done, total, bytes } = event;
  progress = new Map(progress).set(event.instance, { task, done, total, bytes });
  notify();
}

export function clearContentProgress(id: string) {
  if (!progress.has(id)) return;
  const next = new Map(progress);
  next.delete(id);
  progress = next;
  notify();
}

export const contentProgressSnapshot = (id: string): ProgressView | null => progress.get(id) ?? null;

export function useContentProgress(id: string | null): ProgressView | null {
  return useSyncExternalStore(
    (listener) => {
      listeners.add(listener);
      return () => {
        listeners.delete(listener);
      };
    },
    () => (id ? contentProgressSnapshot(id) : null),
  );
}

export function resetContentProgress() {
  progress = new Map();
  notify();
}
```

`ui/src/state/handleEvent.ts`, first lines of `handleEvent`:

```ts
  browseProject: (id: string) => ['browse', 'project', id] as const,
  browseVersions: (id: string) => ['browse', 'versions', id] as const,

```

and the hooks (import `useInfiniteQuery`):

```ts
export const BROWSE_PAGE = 20;
const FIVE_MINUTES = 5 * 60_000;

export const useBrowseSearch = (request: Omit<SearchRequest, 'offset'>) =>
  useInfiniteQuery({
    queryKey: qk.browseSearch(request),
    queryFn: ({ pageParam }) => commands.browseSearch({ ...request, offset: pageParam }),
    initialPageParam: 0,
    // The core drops server-only hits, so a page can be short; step by the page size.
    getNextPageParam: (last) => (last.offset + BROWSE_PAGE < last.total ? last.offset + BROWSE_PAGE : undefined),
    staleTime: FIVE_MINUTES,
  });
export const useBrowseCategories = (kind: ContentKind) =>
  useQuery({ queryKey: qk.browseCategories(kind), queryFn: () => commands.browseCategories(kind), staleTime: Infinity });
export const useBrowseProject = (id: string) =>
  useQuery({ queryKey: qk.browseProject(id), queryFn: () => commands.browseProject(id), staleTime: FIVE_MINUTES });
export const useBrowseVersions = (id: string) =>
  useQuery({ queryKey: qk.browseVersions(id), queryFn: () => commands.browseVersions(id, null), staleTime: FIVE_MINUTES });
export const useBrowseInstalled = (id: string | null) =>
  useQuery({ queryKey: qk.browseInstalled(id ?? ''), queryFn: () => commands.browseInstalled(id as string), enabled: id !== null });
export const useBrowseImage = (url: string | null, kind: ImageKind) =>
  useQuery({
    queryKey: qk.browseImage(url ?? '', kind),
    queryFn: () => commands.browseImage(url as string, kind),
    enabled: url !== null,
    staleTime: Infinity,
  });
```

`ui/src/ui/useDebounced.ts`:

```ts
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

/** `value`, once it has stopped changing for `ms`. The first value is returned at once. */
export function useDebounced<T>(value: T, ms: number): T {
  const [settled, setSettled] = useState(value);
  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = setTimeout(() => setSettled(value), ms);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [value, ms]);
  return settled;
}
```

```ts
/** 81000000 → "81M" (es: "81 M"), for download and follow counts. */
export function formatCompact(n: number, lang: string): string {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat(lang, { notation: 'compact', maximumFractionDigits: 1 }).format(n);
}
```

`ui/src/features/browse/labels.ts`:

```ts
import type { i18n as I18n } from 'i18next';
import type { LoaderKind, VersionInfo } from '../../ipc/schema';
import { loaderName } from '../home/labels';

/** "Fabric 1.20.1", or "Minecraft 1.21.1" without a loader. */
export const gameLabel = (minecraft: string, loader: LoaderKind | null) =>
  loader ? `${loaderName(loader)} ${minecraft}` : `Minecraft ${minecraft}`;

/** A translated category, or the slug made readable ("game-mechanics" → "Game mechanics"). */
export function categoryLabel(i18n: I18n, slug: string): string {
  const key = `categories.${slug}`;
  if (i18n.exists(key)) return i18n.t(key);
  const words = slug.replace(/[-_]+/g, ' ').trim();
  return words.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + words.slice(1);
}

/** "mc1.20.1-0.5.11 · 1.20.1" (the newest few game versions it supports). */
export const versionLabel = (v: VersionInfo) => `${v.number} · ${v.gameVersions.slice(-3).join(', ')}`;
```

`ui/src/features/browse/ProjectIcon.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { PlotIcon } from '../../art/PlotIcon';
import { useBrowseImage } from '../../state/queries';

/** A project's own icon through the core's image cache, or a generated plot icon. */
export function ProjectIcon({ seed, url, size = 48 }: { seed: string; url: string | null; size?: number }) {
  const image = useBrowseImage(url, 'icon');
  const [failed, setFailed] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const src = image.data && image.data !== failed ? image.data : null;
  return src ? (
    <img src={src} alt="" width={size} height={size} className="browse-icon" draggable={false} onError={() => setFailed(src)} />
  ) : (
    <PlotIcon seed={seed} size={size} />
  );
}
```

`ui/src/features/browse/ResultRow.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import type { SearchHit } from '../../ipc/schema';
import { formatCompact } from '../../ui/format';
import { categoryLabel } from './labels';
import { ProjectIcon } from './ProjectIcon';

export function ResultRow({
  hit,
  scoped,
  installed,
  onOpen,
  action,
}: {
  hit: SearchHit;
  /** Browse is adding to one instance: show its lamp or an Install button. */
  scoped: boolean;
  installed: boolean;
  onOpen: () => void;
  action?: ReactNode;
}) {
  const { t, i18n } = useTranslation();
  const lang = i18n.language;
  return (
    <li className="browse-row">
      <button type="button" className="browse-row-open" onClick={onOpen}>
        <ProjectIcon seed={hit.projectId} url={hit.iconUrl} />
        <span className="grid min-w-0 gap-1 text-left">
          <span className="font-display text-lg leading-tight">{hit.title}</span>
          {hit.author && <span className="text-sm text-ink-dim">{t('browse.by', { author: hit.author })}</span>}
          <span className="line-clamp-2 text-ink-dim">{hit.summary}</span>
          <span className="tabular text-sm text-ink-dim">
            {t('browse.downloads', { count: hit.downloads, shown: formatCompact(hit.downloads, lang) })}
            {' · '}
            {t('browse.follows', { count: hit.follows, shown: formatCompact(hit.follows, lang) })}
          </span>
          {hit.categories.length > 0 && (
            <span className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
              {hit.categories.map((c) => (
                <span key={c} className="tag">
                  {categoryLabel(i18n, c)}
                </span>
              ))}
            </span>
          )}
        </span>
      </button>
      {scoped && (installed ? <span className="installed-lamp">{t('browse.installed')}</span> : action)}
    </li>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: The screen**

`ui/src/features/browse/Browse.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { isLauncherError } from '../../ipc/errors';
import type { ContentKind, LoaderKind, SortIndex } from '../../ipc/schema';
import { useBrowseCategories, useBrowseInstalled, useBrowseSearch, useInstances, useMcVersions } from '../../state/queries';
import { navigate, type Route } from '../../state/route';
import { Button } from '../../ui/Button';
import { ErrorCard } from '../../ui/ErrorCard';
import { formatCount } from '../../ui/format';
import { ScreenFrame } from '../../ui/ScreenFrame';
import { Select } from '../../ui/Select';
import { useDebounced } from '../../ui/useDebounced';
import { loaderName } from '../home/labels';
import { categoryLabel, gameLabel } from './labels';
import { ProjectPage } from './ProjectPage';
import { ResultRow } from './ResultRow';
import { remember, sessionFilters, type Filters } from './session';

export type BrowseRoute = Extract<Route, { name: 'browse' }>;

const ALL_KINDS: ContentKind[] = ['modpack', 'mod', 'resource_pack', 'shader'];
const SORTS: SortIndex[] = ['relevance', 'downloads', 'follows', 'newest', 'updated'];
const LOADERS: LoaderKind[] = ['fabric', 'quilt', 'forge', 'neoforge'];
const VISIBLE_CATEGORIES = 8;

export function Browse({ route }: { route: BrowseRoute }) {
  if (route.project) return <ProjectPage key={route.project} route={route} project={route.project} />;
  // A new kind or scope starts from that kind's remembered filters.
  return <BrowseList key={`${route.kind}:${route.instance ?? ''}`} route={route} />;
}

function Sentinel({ onVisible }: { onVisible: () => void }) {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    const el = ref.current;
    if (!el || typeof IntersectionObserver === 'undefined') return;
    const observer = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
      if (entries.some((entry) => entry.isIntersecting)) onVisible();
    });
    observer.observe(el);
    return () => observer.disconnect();
  }, [onVisible]);
  return <div ref={ref} aria-hidden className="h-px" />;
}

function RateLimited({ seconds, onRetry }: { seconds: number; onRetry: () => unknown }) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const [left, setLeft] = useState(seconds);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (left <= 0) {
      void onRetry();
      return;
    }
    const timer = setTimeout(() => setLeft((n) => n - 1), 1000);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [left, onRetry]);
  return (
    <p role="status" className="panel p-4">
      {t('browse.rateLimited', { seconds: Math.max(left, 0) })}
    </p>
  );
}

function BrowseList({ route }: { route: BrowseRoute }) {
  const { t, i18n } = useTranslation();
  const lang = i18n.language;
  const scoped = route.instance !== undefined;
  const instances = useInstances();
  const scope = scoped ? (instances.data?.instances.find((i) => i.id === route.instance) ?? null) : null;
  const [filters, setFilters] = useState<Filters>(() => sessionFilters(route.kind, scoped));
  useEffect(() => remember(route.kind, scoped, filters), [route.kind, scoped, filters]);
  const update = (patch: Partial<Filters>) => setFilters((f) => ({ ...f, ...patch }));

  const query = useDebounced(filters.query.trim(), 300);
  const sort = filters.sort ?? (query === '' ? 'downloads' : 'relevance');
  const usesLoader = route.kind === 'mod' || route.kind === 'modpack';
  const results = useBrowseSearch({
    query,
    kind: route.kind,
    instance: route.instance ?? null,
    minecraft: scoped ? null : filters.minecraft,
    loader: scoped || !usesLoader ? null : filters.loader,
    categories: filters.categories,
    sort,
  });
  const installed = useBrowseInstalled(route.instance ?? null);
  const categories = useBrowseCategories(route.kind);
  const mcVersions = useMcVersions(!scoped);
  const [allChips, setAllChips] = useState(false);

  const searchRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    const onKey = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      const target = event.target as HTMLElement | null;
      if (event.key !== '/' || target?.closest('input, textarea, select, [contenteditable="true"]')) return;
      event.preventDefault();
      searchRef.current?.focus();
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
  }, []);

  const { fetchNextPage } = results;
  const loadMore = useCallback(() => void fetchNextPage(), [fetchNextPage]);

  const kinds = scope
    ? ALL_KINDS.filter((k) => k !== 'modpack' && (scope.game.loader !== null || k === 'resource_pack'))
    : ALL_KINDS;
  const hits = results.data?.pages.flatMap((page) => page.hits) ?? [];
  const total = results.data?.pages[0]?.total ?? 0;
  const chips = categories.data ?? [];
  const shownChips = allChips ? chips : chips.slice(0, VISIBLE_CATEGORIES);
  const toggle = (slug: string) =>
    update({
      categories: filters.categories.includes(slug) ? filters.categories.filter((c) => c !== slug) : [...filters.categories, slug],
    });
  const clear = () => update({ query: '', categories: [], minecraft: null, loader: null });

  let body: ReactNode;
  if (results.isPending) {
    body = <p role="status" className="text-ink-dim">{t('common.loading')}</p>;
  } else if (results.isError && hits.length === 0) {
    const error = results.error;
    if (isLauncherError(error) && error.code === 'host_unreachable') {
      body = (
        <div className="panel flex items-center justify-between gap-4 p-4">
          <p>{t('browse.offline')}</p>
          <Button onClick={() => void results.refetch()}>{t('common.retry')}</Button>
        </div>
      );
    } else if (isLauncherError(error) && error.code === 'browse_rate_limited') {
      body = <RateLimited key={results.errorUpdatedAt} seconds={Number(error.params.seconds ?? '10')} onRetry={results.refetch} />;
    } else {
      body = <ErrorCard error={error} actions={<Button onClick={() => void results.refetch()}>{t('common.retry')}</Button>} />;
    }
  } else if (hits.length === 0) {
    body = (
      <div className="grid justify-items-start gap-3">
        <p>{query ? t('browse.nothing', { query }) : t('browse.empty')}</p>
        {(query || filters.categories.length > 0 || filters.minecraft || filters.loader) && (
          <Button onClick={clear}>{t('browse.clearFilters')}</Button>
        )}
      </div>
    );
  } else {
    body = (
      <>
        <p className="tabular text-sm text-ink-dim">{t('browse.results', { count: total, shown: formatCount(total, lang) })}</p>
        <ul className="browse-list">
          {hits.map((hit) => (
            <ResultRow
              key={hit.projectId}
              hit={hit}
              scoped={scoped}
              installed={installed.data?.includes(hit.projectId) ?? false}
              onOpen={() => navigate({ ...route, project: hit.slug })}
            />
          ))}
        </ul>
        {results.hasNextPage ? (
          <div className="grid justify-items-center gap-2">
            <Sentinel onVisible={loadMore} />
            <Button disabled={results.isFetchingNextPage} onClick={loadMore}>
              {results.isFetchingNextPage ? t('common.loading') : t('browse.loadMore')}
            </Button>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <p className="text-center text-ink-dim">{t('browse.end')}</p>
        )}
      </>
    );
  }

  return (
    <ScreenFrame
      title={t('browse.title')}
      wide
      onBack={scope ? () => navigate({ name: 'mods', id: scope.id }) : undefined}
    >
      <div className="grid gap-5">
        {scope && (
          <div className="browse-scope">
            <p>{t('browse.scope', { name: scope.name, game: gameLabel(scope.game.minecraft, scope.game.loader?.kind ?? null) })}</p>
            <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => navigate({ name: 'browse', kind: route.kind })}>
              {t('browse.dropScope')}
            </Button>
          </div>
        )}
        <div role="tablist" aria-label={t('browse.title')} className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          {kinds.map((kind) => (
            <button
              key={kind}
              type="button"
              role="tab"
              className="seg"
              aria-selected={kind === route.kind}
              data-checked={kind === route.kind ? '' : undefined}
              onClick={() => navigate({ name: 'browse', kind, instance: route.instance })}
            >
              {t(`browse.kinds.${kind}`)}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
        <div className="browse-controls">
          <input
            ref={searchRef}
            type="search"
            className="field-input"
            aria-label={t('browse.search')}
            placeholder={t(`browse.placeholders.${route.kind}`)}
            value={filters.query}
            onChange={(e) => update({ query: e.target.value })}
          />
          <Select
            label={t('browse.sort')}
            value={sort}
            onChange={(value) => update({ sort: value as SortIndex })}
            options={SORTS.map((s) => ({ value: s, label: t(`browse.sorts.${s}`) }))}
          />
          {!scoped && (
            <Select
              label={t('browse.minecraft')}
              value={filters.minecraft ?? ''}
              onChange={(value) => update({ minecraft: value || null })}
              options={[
                { value: '', label: t('browse.anyVersion') },
                ...(mcVersions.data ?? []).filter((v) => v.kind === 'release').map((v) => ({ value: v.id, label: v.id })),
              ]}
            />
          )}
          {!scoped && usesLoader && (
            <Select
              label={t('browse.loader')}
              value={filters.loader ?? ''}
              onChange={(value) => update({ loader: (value || null) as LoaderKind | null })}
              options={[{ value: '', label: t('browse.anyLoader') }, ...LOADERS.map((l) => ({ value: l, label: loaderName(l) }))]}
            />
          )}
        </div>
        {chips.length > 0 && (
          <fieldset className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            <legend className="sr-only">{t('browse.categories')}</legend>
            {shownChips.map((slug) => (
              <button key={slug} type="button" className="chip" aria-pressed={filters.categories.includes(slug)} onClick={() => toggle(slug)}>
                {categoryLabel(i18n, slug)}
              </button>
            ))}
            {chips.length > VISIBLE_CATEGORIES && (
              <button type="button" className="chip-more" onClick={() => setAllChips((v) => !v)}>
                {allChips ? t('browse.fewerCategories') : t('browse.moreCategories')}
              </button>
            )}
          </fieldset>
        )}
        {body}
      </div>
    </ScreenFrame>
  );
}
```

(Check the `McVersion` schema's kind values in `ui/src/ipc/schema.ts`; if releases aren't `'release'`, use the existing filter from `ui/src/features/new-instance/useVersionChoice.ts`.)

```tsx
import { screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { expect, it } from 'vitest';
import projectFx from '../../ipc/contract/project_detail.json';
import type { ProjectDetail } from '../../ipc/schema';
import { routeSnapshot } from '../../state/route';
import { renderApp, sent } from '../../test/backend';
import { renderWithProviders } from '../../test/render';
import { Browse } from './Browse';

const route = { name: 'browse' as const, kind: 'mod' as const, project: 'sodium' };

it('shows the project, its gallery and its links', async () => {
  const { user, calls } = renderApp(<Browse route={route} />);
  expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Sodium' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  expect(screen.getByText('by jellysquid3')).toBeInTheDocument();
  expect(screen.queryByText('The server needs it too')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Before and after' }));
  const dialog = await screen.findByRole('dialog');
  expect(within(dialog).getByRole('img')).toHaveAttribute('src', expect.stringMatching(/^data:image\//));
  await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Close' }));
  await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Source code' }));
  expect(sent(calls, 'open_link')).toEqual([{ url: 'https://github.com/CaffeineMC/sodium' }]);
  await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Back to results' }));
  expect(routeSnapshot()).toEqual({ name: 'browse', kind: 'mod', instance: undefined });
});

it('renders the description without raw HTML or foreign images', async () => {
  const detail: ProjectDetail = {
    ...(projectFx as ProjectDetail),
    serverSide: 'required',
    body: [
      '# Sodium',
      'Text <b>bold</b> <script>alert(1)</script>',
      '[Wiki](https://example.com/wiki) [bad](javascript:alert(1)) [plain](http://example.com)',
      '![shot](https://cdn.modrinth.com/data/x/shot.png) ![tracker](https://i.imgur.com/x.png)',
    ].join('\n\n'),
  };
  const opened: unknown[] = [];
  const { user, container } = renderWithProviders(<Browse route={route} />, {
    ipc: (cmd, args) => {
      if (cmd === 'browse_project') return detail;
      if (cmd === 'browse_image') return 'data:image/png;base64,AAAA';
      if (cmd === 'browse_versions' || cmd === 'browse_installed') return [];
      if (cmd === 'instances_list') return { instances: [], damaged: [] };
      if (cmd === 'open_link') opened.push(args);
      return null;
    },
  });
  expect(await screen.findByText('The server needs it too')).toBeInTheDocument();
  const article = container.querySelector('.markdown')!;
  expect(article.querySelector('b, script')).toBeNull();
  expect(within(article as HTMLElement).getByRole('img', { name: 'shot' })).toHaveAttribute('src', 'data:image/png;base64,AAAA');
  expect(within(article as HTMLElement).queryByRole('img', { name: 'tracker' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  expect(within(article as HTMLElement).getByText('tracker')).toBeInTheDocument();
  expect(within(article as HTMLElement).queryByRole('button', { name: 'bad' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  expect(within(article as HTMLElement).queryByRole('button', { name: 'plain' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  await user.click(within(article as HTMLElement).getByRole('button', { name: 'Wiki' }));
  expect(opened).toEqual([{ url: 'https://example.com/wiki' }]);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Markdown**

`ui/src/features/browse/ProjectMarkdown.tsx`:

```tsx
// A project's description. Raw HTML is skipped (no rehype-raw), images show only from the CDN
// through the core's image cache, and only https links open (in the system browser, from Rust).
import Markdown from 'react-markdown';
import remarkGfm from 'remark-gfm';
import { commands } from '../../ipc/commands';
import { useBrowseImage } from '../../state/queries';
import { pushError } from '../../state/toasts';

export function isCdnImage(src: string): boolean {
  try {
    const url = new URL(src);
    return url.protocol === 'https:' && url.host === 'cdn.modrinth.com' && !url.username && !url.password;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

function MarkdownImage({ src, alt }: { src: string; alt: string }) {
  const image = useBrowseImage(src, 'gallery');
  return image.data ? <img src={image.data} alt={alt} className="md-img" /> : <span className="md-alt">{alt}</span>;
}

export function ProjectMarkdown({ source }: { source: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="markdown">
      <Markdown
        remarkPlugins={[remarkGfm]}
        skipHtml
        components={{
          a: ({ href, children }) =>
            href?.startsWith('https://') ? (
              <button type="button" className="md-link" onClick={() => void commands.openLink(href).catch(pushError)}>
                {children}
              </button>
            ) : (
              <span>{children}</span>
            ),
          img: ({ src, alt }) =>
            typeof src === 'string' && isCdnImage(src) ? (
              <MarkdownImage src={src} alt={alt ?? ''} />
            ) : alt ? (
              <span className="md-alt">{alt}</span>
            ) : null,
        }}
      >
        {source}
      </Markdown>
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Gallery**

`ui/src/features/browse/Gallery.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import type { GalleryImage } from '../../ipc/schema';
import { useBrowseImage } from '../../state/queries';
import { Button } from '../../ui/Button';
import { Dialog } from '../../ui/Dialog';

function Picture({ url, alt, large = false }: { url: string; alt: string; large?: boolean }) {
  const image = useBrowseImage(url, 'gallery');
  return image.data ? (
    <img src={image.data} alt={alt} className={large ? 'gallery-full' : 'gallery-thumb'} draggable={false} />
  ) : (
    <span className={`${large ? 'gallery-full' : 'gallery-thumb'} gallery-empty`} />
  );
}

export function Gallery({ images }: { images: GalleryImage[] }) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const [open, setOpen] = useState<number | null>(null);
  if (images.length === 0) return null;
  const name = (i: number) => images[i]?.title ?? t('project.galleryImage', { n: i + 1, total: images.length });
  const current = open === null ? null : images[open];
  return (
    <section aria-label={t('project.gallery')}>
      <ul className="flex gap-3 overflow-x-auto pb-2">
        {images.map((image, i) => (
          <li key={image.url} className="flex-none">
            <button type="button" className="gallery-open" aria-label={name(i)} onClick={() => setOpen(i)}>
              <Picture url={image.url} alt="" />
            </button>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
      <Dialog
        open={current !== null && current !== undefined}
        onClose={() => setOpen(null)}
        title={open === null ? '' : name(open)}
        actions={<Button onClick={() => setOpen(null)}>{t('common.close')}</Button>}
      >
        {current && <Picture url={current.url} alt={name(open!)} large />}
      </Dialog>
    </section>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: The page**

`ui/src/features/browse/ProjectPage.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { commands } from '../../ipc/commands';
import type { ProjectDetail } from '../../ipc/schema';
import { useBrowseProject } from '../../state/queries';
import { navigate } from '../../state/route';
import { pushError } from '../../state/toasts';
import { Button } from '../../ui/Button';
import { ErrorCard } from '../../ui/ErrorCard';
import { formatCompact } from '../../ui/format';
import { ExternalLink } from '../../ui/Icon';
import { ScreenFrame } from '../../ui/ScreenFrame';
import type { BrowseRoute } from './Browse';
import { Gallery } from './Gallery';
import { categoryLabel } from './labels';
import { ProjectIcon } from './ProjectIcon';
import { ProjectMarkdown } from './ProjectMarkdown';

function ProjectLinks({ project }: { project: ProjectDetail }) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const links = (
    [
      ['source', project.sourceUrl],
      ['issues', project.issuesUrl],
      ['wiki', project.wikiUrl],
    ] as const
  ).filter((entry): entry is readonly ['source' | 'issues' | 'wiki', string] => entry[1] !== null);
  if (links.length === 0 && !project.license) return null;
  return (
    <section className="grid gap-2" aria-label={t('project.links')}>
      <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold">{t('project.links')}</h2>
      <ul className="grid justify-items-start gap-1">
        {links.map(([key, url]) => (
          <li key={key}>
            <Button variant="ghost" icon={<ExternalLink />} onClick={() => void commands.openLink(url).catch(pushError)}>
              {t(`project.${key}`)}
            </Button>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
      {project.license && <p className="text-sm text-ink-dim">{t('project.license', { license: project.license })}</p>}
    </section>
  );
}

export function ProjectPage({ route, project }: { route: BrowseRoute; project: string }) {
  const { t, i18n } = useTranslation();
  const detail = useBrowseProject(project);
  const back = () => navigate({ name: 'browse', kind: route.kind, instance: route.instance });
  const frame = (title: string, children: ReactNode, subtitle?: string) => (
    <ScreenFrame title={title} subtitle={subtitle} wide onBack={back} backLabel={t('project.back')}>
      {children}
    </ScreenFrame>
  );
  if (detail.isPending) return frame(t('browse.title'), <p role="status">{t('common.loading')}</p>);
  if (detail.isError)
    return frame(
      t('browse.title'),
      <ErrorCard error={detail.error} actions={<Button onClick={() => void detail.refetch()}>{t('common.retry')}</Button>} />,
    );
  const p = detail.data;
  const lang = i18n.language;
  const installSlot = null; // Task 21: <ActionPlate project={p} route={route} />
  return frame(
    p.title,
    <div className="project-layout">
      <div className="grid min-w-0 content-start gap-6">
        <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
          <ProjectIcon seed={p.projectId} url={p.iconUrl} size={64} />
          <div className="grid gap-2">
            <p>{p.summary}</p>
            <p className="tabular text-sm text-ink-dim">
              {t('browse.downloads', { count: p.downloads, shown: formatCompact(p.downloads, lang) })}
              {' · '}
              {t('browse.follows', { count: p.follows, shown: formatCompact(p.follows, lang) })}
            </p>
            {p.categories.length > 0 && (
              <p className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                {p.categories.map((c) => (
                  <span key={c} className="tag">
                    {categoryLabel(i18n, c)}
                  </span>
                ))}
              </p>
            )}
            {p.serverSide === 'required' && <p className="text-sm text-blue-ink">{t('project.serverToo')}</p>}
          </div>
        </div>
        <Gallery images={p.gallery} />
        <ProjectMarkdown source={p.body} />
      </div>
      <aside className="grid content-start gap-6">
        {installSlot}
        <ProjectLinks project={p} />
      </aside>
    </div>,
    p.author ? t('browse.by', { author: p.author }) : undefined,
  );
}
```

```css
  }

    }
  }

  }
```

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { routeSnapshot } from '../../state/route';

    <Toasts />

);

it('confirms a mod that brings a dependency, then installs it', async () => {
  const { user, calls } = renderApp(page('iris', 'asalith-fields'));
  await user.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Install' }));
  const dialog = await screen.findByRole('dialog', { name: 'Add Iris to Asalith Fields' });
  expect(within(dialog).getByText(/Sodium/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  expect(within(dialog).getByText('needed by Iris')).toBeInTheDocument();
  expect(within(dialog).getByText('Already have: Fabric API')).toBeInTheDocument();
  expect(sent(calls, 'browse_plan')).toEqual([
    { request: { project: 'YL57xq9U', version: 'YL57xq9U-v1', target: { kind: 'instance', id: 'asalith-fields' } } },

  await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Install' }));

  expect(sent(calls, 'browse_install')).toEqual([{ planId: 'plan-1' }]);
  expect(await screen.findByText('Already in Asalith Fields')).toBeInTheDocument();
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The flow**

`ui/src/features/browse/useInstallFlow.ts`:

```ts
import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { commands } from '../../ipc/commands';
import type { BrowsePlanRequest, InstallPlanView, InstallResult } from '../../ipc/schema';
import { clearContentProgress } from '../../state/contentProgress';
import { qk } from '../../state/queries';
import { navigate } from '../../state/route';
import { runStore } from '../../state/runStore';
import { pushError, pushNotice } from '../../state/toasts';

/** Ask first when the install adds more than the one thing, skips something, or makes an instance. */
export const needsConfirm = (view: InstallPlanView) =>
  view.items.length > 1 || view.skipped.length > 0 || view.target.kind === 'new';

export function useInstallFlow() {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const [confirming, setConfirming] = useState<InstallPlanView | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  const finish = (view: InstallPlanView, result: InstallResult) => {
    const id = result.instance.id;
    clearContentProgress(id);
    for (const queryKey of [qk.instances, qk.browseInstalled(id), qk.mods(id), qk.packContents(id)]) {
      void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey });
    }
    const first = view.items[0];
    const name = first?.title ?? '';
    const more = view.items.length - 1;
    const instance = result.instance.name;
    const state = runStore.run(id).state;
    const playing = state === 'preparing' || state === 'running' || state === 'stopping';
    pushNotice({
      title: more > 0 ? t('install.addedMore', { count: more, name, instance }) : t('install.added', { name, instance }),
      body: playing
        ? t('install.restart')
        : first?.kind === 'resource_pack'
          ? t('install.turnOnResourcePack')
          : first?.kind === 'shader'
            ? t('install.turnOnShader')
            : undefined,
      action: { label: t('install.open'), run: () => navigate({ name: 'home', id }) },
    });
    if (view.target.kind === 'new') navigate({ name: 'home', id });
  };

  const install = async (view: InstallPlanView) => {
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      finish(view, await commands.browseInstall(view.planId));
    } catch (error) {
      pushError(error);
    } finally {
      setConfirming(null);
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  const start = async (request: BrowsePlanRequest) => {
    setBusy(true);
    let view: InstallPlanView;
    try {
      view = await commands.browsePlan(request);
    } catch (error) {
      pushError(error);
      setBusy(false);
      return;
    }
    if (needsConfirm(view)) {
      setConfirming(view);
      setBusy(false);
      return;
    }
    await install(view);
  };

  return { busy, confirming, start, install, cancel: () => setConfirming(null) };
}
```

`ui/src/features/browse/InstallDialog.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import type { InstallPlanView } from '../../ipc/schema';
import { Button } from '../../ui/Button';
import { Dialog } from '../../ui/Dialog';
import { formatBytes } from '../../ui/format';
import { gameLabel } from './labels';

export function InstallDialog({
  view,
  busy,
  onConfirm,
  onClose,
}: {
  view: InstallPlanView;
  busy: boolean;
  onConfirm: () => void;
  onClose: () => void;
}) {
  const { t, i18n } = useTranslation();
  const name = view.items[0]?.title ?? '';
  const title =
    view.target.kind === 'new'
      ? t('install.titleNew', { name, game: gameLabel(view.target.minecraft, view.target.loader) })
      : t('install.titleExisting', { name, instance: view.target.name });
  return (
    <Dialog
      open
      onClose={onClose}
      title={title}
      actions={
        <>
          <Button onClick={onClose}>{t('common.cancel')}</Button>
          <Button variant="primary" disabled={busy} onClick={onConfirm}>
            {busy ? t('project.installing') : t('install.confirm')}
          </Button>
        </>
      }
    >
      <div className="grid gap-3">
        <ul className="grid gap-1">
          {view.items.map((item) => (
            <li key={item.projectId}>
              {item.reason.kind === 'requested' ? (
                <strong>{item.title}</strong>
              ) : (
                <>
                  {`+ ${item.title} · `}
                  <span className="text-ink-dim">
                    {item.reason.kind === 'dependency' ? t('install.neededBy', { name: item.reason.of }) : t('install.forShaders')}
                  </span>
                </>
              )}
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
        {view.skipped.length > 0 && (
          <p className="text-ink-dim">
            {t('install.alreadyHave', {
              names: view.skipped.map((s) => (s.reason === 'in_pack' ? t('install.inPack', { name: s.title }) : s.title)).join(', '),
            })}
          </p>
        )}
        <p className="tabular text-sm text-ink-dim">{t('install.total', { size: formatBytes(view.totalSize, i18n.language) })}</p>
      </div>
    </Dialog>
  );
}
```

(`Dialog` must give the `<dialog>` an accessible name from `title`; it does, through `aria-labelledby`.)

- [ ] **Step 4: The plates**

`ui/src/features/browse/ActionPlate.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useId, useState } from 'react';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { taskLabel } from '../../ipc/errors';
import type { ProjectDetail } from '../../ipc/schema';
import { useContentProgress } from '../../state/contentProgress';
import { useAddPack, useBrowseInstalled, useBrowseVersions, useInstances } from '../../state/queries';
import { navigate } from '../../state/route';
import { Button } from '../../ui/Button';
import { Meter } from '../../ui/Meter';
import { Select } from '../../ui/Select';
import type { BrowseRoute } from './Browse';
import { fitFor, fitsVersion, pickDefault } from './compat';
import { InstallDialog } from './InstallDialog';
import { versionLabel } from './labels';
import { useInstallFlow } from './useInstallFlow';

const NEW = '__new__';

function ModpackPlate({ project }: { project: ProjectDetail }) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const versions = useBrowseVersions(project.projectId);
  const add = useAddPack();
  const [chosen, setChosen] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const list = versions.data ?? [];
  const version = chosen ?? pickDefault(list)?.id ?? '';
  return (
    <div className="panel grid gap-4 p-5">
      <Select
        label={t('project.version')}
        value={version}
        onChange={setChosen}
        disabled={list.length === 0}
        options={list.map((v) => ({ value: v.id, label: versionLabel(v) }))}
      />
      <Button
        variant="primary"
        size="lg"
        disabled={!version || add.isPending}
        onClick={() =>
          add.mutate(
            { kind: 'link', url: `modrinth:${project.projectId}@${version}` },
            { onSuccess: (instance) => navigate({ name: 'home', id: instance.id }) },
          )
        }
      >
        {add.isPending ? t('project.creating') : t('project.createInstance')}
      </Button>
    </div>
  );
}

function ContentPlate({ project, route }: { project: ProjectDetail; route: BrowseRoute }) {
  const { t, i18n } = useTranslation();
  const targetId = useId();
  const versions = useBrowseVersions(project.projectId);
  const instances = useInstances();
  const all = versions.data ?? [];
  const list = instances.data?.instances ?? [];
  const fits = new Map(list.map((i) => [i.id, fitFor(all, project.kind, i)] as const));
  const firstFit = list.find((i) => fits.get(i.id)?.ok)?.id;
  const [picked, setPicked] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [chosen, setChosen] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const target = picked ?? route.instance ?? firstFit ?? NEW;
  const instance = list.find((i) => i.id === target) ?? null;
  const options = instance ? all.filter((v) => fitsVersion(v, project.kind, instance)) : all;
  const version = options.some((v) => v.id === chosen) ? chosen : (pickDefault(options)?.id ?? null);
  const installed = useBrowseInstalled(instance?.id ?? null);
  const already = installed.data?.includes(project.projectId) ?? false;
  const flow = useInstallFlow();
  const progress = useContentProgress(instance?.id ?? null);
  const reason = (id: string) => {
    const fit = fits.get(id);
    if (!fit || fit.ok || versions.isPending) return null;
    return t(fit.reason === 'needsLoader' ? 'project.needsLoader' : 'project.noVersion');
  };
  return (
    <div className="panel grid gap-4 p-5">
      <div className="grid gap-1.5">
        <label htmlFor={targetId} className="text-sm font-semibold">
          {t('project.installTo')}
        </label>
        <select
          id={targetId}
          className="field-input"
          value={target}
          onChange={(e) => {
            setPicked(e.target.value);
            setChosen(null);
          }}
        >
          {list.map((i) => {
            const why = reason(i.id);
            return (
              <option key={i.id} value={i.id} disabled={why !== null}>
                {why ? `${i.name} — ${why}` : i.name}
              </option>
            );
          })}
          <option value={NEW}>{t('project.newInstance')}</option>
        </select>
      </div>
      {options.length > 0 ? (
        <Select
          label={t('project.version')}
          value={version ?? ''}
          onChange={setChosen}
          options={options.map((v) => ({ value: v.id, label: versionLabel(v) }))}
        />
      ) : (
        !versions.isPending && <p className="text-ink-dim">{t('project.noVersions')}</p>
      )}
      {already && instance ? (
        <p className="installed-lamp">{t('project.alreadyThere', { name: instance.name })}</p>
      ) : (
        <Button
          variant="primary"
          size="lg"
          disabled={flow.busy || version === null}
          onClick={() =>
            void flow.start({
              project: project.projectId,
              version,
              target: instance ? { kind: 'instance', id: instance.id } : { kind: 'new' },
            })
          }
        >
          {flow.busy ? t('project.installing') : t('project.install')}
        </Button>
      )}
      {flow.busy && progress && (
        <Meter value={progress.total > 0 ? progress.done / progress.total : 0} label={taskLabel(i18n, progress.task)} />
      )}
      {flow.confirming && (
        <InstallDialog
          view={flow.confirming}
          busy={flow.busy}
          onConfirm={() => void flow.install(flow.confirming!)}
          onClose={flow.cancel}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export function ActionPlate({ project, route }: { project: ProjectDetail; route: BrowseRoute }) {
  return project.kind === 'modpack' ? <ModpackPlate project={project} /> : <ContentPlate project={project} route={route} />;
}
```

`ui/src/features/browse/RowInstall.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import type { SearchHit } from '../../ipc/schema';
import { Button } from '../../ui/Button';
import { InstallDialog } from './InstallDialog';
import { useInstallFlow } from './useInstallFlow';

/** Install from a scoped result row: newest fitting version, same confirm rules. Never lit. */
export function RowInstall({ hit, instanceId }: { hit: SearchHit; instanceId: string }) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const flow = useInstallFlow();
  return (
    <>
      <Button
        disabled={flow.busy}
        onClick={() => void flow.start({ project: hit.projectId, version: null, target: { kind: 'instance', id: instanceId } })}
      >
        {flow.busy ? t('project.installing') : t('browse.install')}
      </Button>
      {flow.confirming && (
        <InstallDialog view={flow.confirming} busy={flow.busy} onConfirm={() => void flow.install(flow.confirming!)} onClose={flow.cancel} />
      )}
    </>
  );
}
```

In `ProjectPage.tsx`, replace `const installSlot = null; …` with `const installSlot = <ActionPlate project={p} route={route} />;` (import it). In `Browse.tsx`, pass `action={scope ? <RowInstall hit={hit} instanceId={scope.id} /> : undefined}` to each `ResultRow`.
